## Chapter 1

<sup>1</sup> Blessed _is_ the man that walketh not in the counsel of the ungodly,<sup>a</sup> nor standeth in the way of sinners,<br>Nor sitteth in the seat of the scornful.<br>
<sup>2</sup> But his delight _is_ in the law of the LORD;<br>And in his law doth he meditate day and night.<br>
<sup>3</sup> And he shall be like a tree planted by the rivers of water,<br>That bringeth forth his fruit in his season;<br>His leaf also shall not wither;<sup>b</sup><br>And whatsoever he doeth shall prosper.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The ungodly _are_ not so:<br>But _are_ like the chaff which the wind driveth away.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Therefore the ungodly shall not stand in the judgment,<br>Nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.<br>
<sup>6</sup> For the LORD knoweth the way of the righteous:<br>But the way of the ungodly shall perish.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _wicked_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Heb. _fade_

## Chapter 2

<sup>1</sup> Why do the heathen rage,<sup>a</sup><br>And the people imagine<sup>b</sup> a vain thing?<br>
<sup>2</sup> The kings of the earth set themselves, and the rulers take counsel together,<br>Against the LORD, and against his anointed, _saying_,<br>
<sup>3</sup> Let us break their bands asunder,<br>And cast away their cords from us.<br>
<sup>4</sup> He that sitteth in the heavens shall laugh:<br>The Lord shall have them in derision.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Then shall he speak unto them in his wrath,<br>And vex<sup>c</sup> them in his sore displeasure.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Yet have I set my king<sup>d</sup> upon my holy hill of Zion.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>7</sup> I will declare the decree:<sup>f</sup> the LORD hath said unto me,<br>Thou _art_ my Son; this day have I begotten thee.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Ask of me, and I shall give _thee_ the heathen _for_ thine inheritance,<br>And the uttermost parts of the earth _for_ thy possession.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Thou shalt break them with a rod of iron;<br>Thou shalt dash them in pieces like a potter’s vessel.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Be wise now therefore, O ye kings:<br>Be instructed, ye judges of the earth.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Serve the LORD with fear,<br>And rejoice with trembling.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Kiss the Son, lest he be angry, and ye perish _from_ the way,<br>When his wrath is kindled but a little. Blessed _are_ all they that put their trust in him.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _tumultuously assemble?_
> - <sup>b</sup> 1. Heb. _mediate_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Or, _trouble_
> - <sup>d</sup> 6. Heb. _anointed_
> - <sup>e</sup> 6. Heb. _upon Zion, the hill of my Holiness_
> - <sup>f</sup> 7. Or, _for a decree_

## Chapter 3

> _A Psalm of David, when he fled from Absalom his son._

<sup>1</sup> LORD, how are they increased that trouble me!<br>Many _are_ they that rise up against me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Many _there be_ which say of my soul,<br>_There is_ no help for him in God. _Selah._<br>
<sup>3</sup> But thou, O LORD, _art_ a shield for<sup>a</sup> me;<br>My glory, and the lifter up of mine head.<br>
<sup>4</sup> I cried unto the LORD with my voice,<br>And he heard me out of his holy hill. _Selah._<br>
<sup>5</sup> I laid me down and slept;<br>I awaked; for the LORD sustained me.<br>
<sup>6</sup> I will not be afraid of ten thousands of people,<br>That have set _themselves_ against me round about.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Arise, O LORD; save me, O my God:<br>For thou hast smitten all mine enemies _upon_ the cheek bone;<br>Thou hast broken the teeth of the ungodly.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Salvation _belongeth_ unto the LORD:<br>Thy blessing _is_ upon thy people. _Selah._<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _about_

## Chapter 4

> _To the chief Musician on Neginoth, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Hear me when I call, O God of my righteousness:<br>Thou hast enlarged me _when I was_ in distress; Have mercy upon me,<sup>a</sup> and hear my prayer.<br>
<sup>2</sup> O ye sons of men, how long _will ye turn_ my glory into shame?<br>_How long_ will ye love vanity, _and_ seek after leasing? _Selah._<br>
<sup>3</sup> But know that the LORD hath set apart him that is godly for himself:<br>The LORD will hear when I call unto him.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Stand in awe, and sin not:<br>Commune with your own heart upon your bed, and be still. _Selah._<br>
<sup>5</sup> Offer the sacrifices of righteousness,<br>And put your trust in the LORD.<br>
<sup>6</sup> _There be_ many that say,<br>Who will shew us _any_ good?<br>LORD, lift thou up<br>The light of thy countenance upon us.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Thou hast put gladness in my heart,<br>More than in the time _that_ their corn and their wine increased.<br>
<sup>8</sup> I will both lay me down in peace, and sleep:<br>For thou, LORD, only makest me dwell in safety.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _be gracious unto me_

## Chapter 5

> _To the chief Musician upon Nehiloth, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Give ear to my words, O LORD,<br>Consider my meditation.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Hearken unto the voice of my cry, my King, and my God:<br>For unto thee will I pray.<br>
<sup>3</sup> My voice shalt thou hear in the morning, O LORD;<br>In the morning will I direct _my prayer_ unto thee, and will look up.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For thou _art_ not a God that hath pleasure in wickedness:<br>Neither shall evil dwell with thee.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The foolish shall not stand in thy sight:<sup>a</sup><br>Thou hatest all workers of iniquity.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Thou shalt destroy them that speak leasing:<br>The LORD will abhor the bloody and deceitful man.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>7</sup> But as for me, I will come _into_ thy house in the multitude of thy mercy:<br>_And_ in thy fear will I worship toward thy holy temple.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>8</sup> Lead me, O LORD, in thy righteousness because of mine enemies;<sup>d</sup><br>Make thy way straight before my face.<br>
<sup>9</sup> For _there is_ no faithfulness<sup>e</sup> in their mouth;<sup>f</sup><br>Their inward part _is_ very wickedness;<sup>g</sup><br>Their throat _is_ an open sepulchre;<br>They flatter with their tongue.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Destroy thou them,<sup>h</sup> O God; let them fall by their own counsels;<sup>i</sup><br>Cast them out in the multitude of their transgressions; for they have rebelled against thee.<br>
<sup>11</sup> But let all those that put their trust in thee rejoice:<br>Let them ever shout for joy, because thou defendest them:<sup>j</sup> let them also that love thy name be joyful in thee.<br>
<sup>12</sup> For thou, LORD, wilt bless the righteous;<br>With favour wilt thou compass him<sup>k</sup> as _with_ a shield.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Heb. _before thine eyes_
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Heb. _man of bloods and deceit_
> - <sup>c</sup> 7. Heb. _the temple of thy holiness_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Heb. _those which observe me_
> - <sup>e</sup> 9. Or, _steadfastness_
> - <sup>f</sup> 9. Heb. _in his mouth i. in the mouth of any of them_
> - <sup>g</sup> 9. Heb. _wickedness_
> - <sup>h</sup> 10. Or, _make them guilty_
> - <sup>i</sup> 10. Or, _from their counsels_
> - <sup>j</sup> 11. Heb. _thou coverest over_, or _protectest them_
> - <sup>k</sup> 12. Heb. _crown him_

## Chapter 6

> _To the chief Musician on Neginoth upon Sheminith, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> O LORD, rebuke me not in thine anger,<br>Neither chasten me in thy hot displeasure.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Have mercy upon me, O LORD; for I _am_ weak:<br>O LORD, heal me; for my bones are vexed.<br>
<sup>3</sup> My soul is also sore vexed:<br>But thou, O LORD, how long?<br>
<sup>4</sup> Return, O LORD, deliver my soul:<br>Oh save me for thy mercies’ sake.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For in death _there is_ no remembrance of thee:<br>In the grave who shall give thee thanks?<br>
<sup>6</sup> I am weary with my groaning; all the night<sup>a</sup> make I my bed to swim;<br>I water my couch with my tears.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Mine eye is consumed because of grief;<br>It waxeth old because of all mine enemies.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Depart from me, all ye workers of iniquity;<br>For the LORD hath heard the voice of my weeping.<br>
<sup>9</sup> The LORD hath heard my supplication;<br>The LORD will receive my prayer.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let all mine enemies be ashamed and sore vexed:<br>Let them return _and_ be ashamed suddenly.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Or, _every night_

## Chapter 7

> _Shiggaion of David, which he sang unto the LORD, concerning the words of Cush the Benjamite._

<sup>1</sup> O LORD my God, in thee do I put my trust:<br>Save me from all them that persecute me, and deliver me:<br>
<sup>2</sup> Lest he tear my soul like a lion, rending _it_ in pieces,<br>While _there is_ none to deliver.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> O LORD my God, if I have done this;<br>If there be iniquity in my hands;<br>
<sup>4</sup> If I have rewarded evil unto him that was at peace with me;<br>(Yea, I have delivered him that without cause is mine enemy:)<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let the enemy persecute my soul, and take _it_;<br>Yea, let him tread down my life upon the earth, and lay mine honour in the dust. _Selah._<br>
<sup>6</sup> Arise, O LORD, in thine anger, lift up thyself because of the rage of mine enemies:<br>And awake for me _to_ the judgment _that_ thou hast commanded.<br>
<sup>7</sup> So shall the congregation of the people compass thee about:<br>For their sakes therefore return thou on high.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The LORD shall judge the people: Judge me, O LORD,<br>According to my righteousness, and according to mine integrity _that is_ in me.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Oh let the wickedness of the wicked come to an end;<br>but establish the just:<br>For the righteous God<br>Trieth the hearts and reins.<br>
<sup>10</sup> My defence _is_ of God,<br>Which saveth the upright in heart.<br>
<sup>11</sup> God judgeth the righteous,<sup>b</sup><br>And God is angry _with the wicked_ every day.<br>
<sup>12</sup> If he turn not, he will whet his sword;<br>He hath bent his bow, and made it ready.<br>
<sup>13</sup> He hath also prepared for him the instruments of death;<br>He ordaineth his arrows against the persecutors.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Behold, he travaileth with iniquity,<br>And hath conceived mischief, and brought forth falsehood.<br>
<sup>15</sup> He made a pit, and digged it,<br>And is fallen into the ditch _which_ he made.<br>
<sup>16</sup> His mischief shall return upon his own head,<br>And his violent dealing shall come down upon his own pate.<br>
<sup>17</sup> I will praise the LORD according to his righteousness:<br>And will sing praise to the name of the LORD most high.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _not a deliverer_
> - <sup>b</sup> 11. Or, _God is a righteous Judge_

## Chapter 8

> _To the chief Musician upon Gittith, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> O LORD our Lord, how excellent _is_ thy name in all the earth!<br>Who hast set thy glory above the heavens.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Out of the mouth of babes and sucklings hast thou ordained<sup>a</sup> strength because of thine enemies,<br>That thou mightest still the enemy and the avenger.<br>
<sup>3</sup> When I consider thy heavens, the work of thy fingers,<br>The moon and the stars, which thou hast ordained;<br>
<sup>4</sup> What is man, that thou art mindful of him?<br>And the son of man, that thou visitest him?<br>
<sup>5</sup> For thou hast made him a little lower than the angels,<br>And hast crowned him with glory and honour.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Thou madest him to have dominion over the works of thy hands;<br>Thou hast put all _things_ under his feet:<br>
<sup>7</sup> All sheep and oxen,<sup>b</sup><br>Yea, and the beasts of the field;<br>
<sup>8</sup> The fowl of the air, and the fish of the sea,<br>_And whatsoever_ passeth through the paths of the seas.<br>
<sup>9</sup> O LORD our Lord,<br>How excellent _is_ thy name in all the earth!<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _founded_
> - <sup>b</sup> 7. Heb. _flocks and oxen, all of them_

## Chapter 9

> _To the chief Musician upon Muth-labben, A Psalm of David._

#### א Aleph

<sup>1</sup> I will praise _thee_, O LORD, with my whole heart;<br>I will shew forth all thy marvellous works.<br>
<sup>2</sup> I will be glad and rejoice in thee:<br>I will sing praise to thy name, O thou Most High.<br>

#### ב Bet
<sup>3</sup> When mine enemies are turned back,<br>They shall fall and perish at thy presence.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For thou hast maintained my right<sup>a</sup> and my cause;<br>Thou satest in the throne judging right.<sup>b</sup><br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>5</sup> Thou hast rebuked the heathen, thou hast destroyed the wicked,<br>Thou hast put out their name for ever and ever.<br>
<sup>6</sup> O thou enemy, destructions are come to a perpetual end:<br>And thou hast destroyed cities; Their memorial is perished with them.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>7</sup> But the LORD shall endure for ever:<br>He hath prepared his throne for judgment.<br>
<sup>8</sup> And he shall judge the world in righteousness,<br>He shall minister judgment to the people in uprightness.<br>

#### ו Waw
<sup>9</sup> The LORD also will be a refuge<sup>c</sup> for the oppressed,<br>A refuge in times of trouble.<br>
<sup>10</sup> And they that know thy name will put their trust in thee:<br>For thou, LORD, hast not forsaken them that seek thee.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>11</sup> Sing praises to the LORD, which dwelleth in Zion:<br>Declare among the people his doings.<br>
<sup>12</sup> When he maketh inquisition for blood, he remembereth them:<br>He forgetteth not the cry of the humble.<sup>d</sup><br>

#### ח Het
<sup>13</sup> Have mercy upon me, O LORD; Consider my trouble _which I suffer_ of them that hate me,<br>Thou that liftest me up from the gates of death:<br>
<sup>14</sup> That I may shew forth all thy praise in the gates of the daughter of Zion:<br>I will rejoice in thy salvation.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>15</sup> The heathen are sunk down in the pit _that_ they made:<br>In the net which they hid is their own foot taken.<br>
<sup>16</sup> The LORD is known _by_ the judgment _which_ he executeth:<br>The wicked is snared in the work of his own hands. _Higgaion._ _Selah._<br>

#### י Yod
<sup>17</sup> The wicked shall be turned into hell,<br>_And_ all the nations that forget God.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>18</sup> For the needy shall not alway be forgotten:<br>The expectation of the poor shall _not_ perish for ever.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Arise, O LORD; let not man prevail:<br>Let the heathen be judged in thy sight.<br>
<sup>20</sup> Put them in fear, O LORD:<br>_That_ the nations may know themselves _to be but_ men. _Selah._<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Heb. _thou hast made my judgment_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Heb. _in righteousness_
> - <sup>c</sup> 9. Heb. _an high place_
> - <sup>d</sup> 12. Or, _afflicted_

## Chapter 10

#### ל Lamed

<sup>1</sup> Why standest thou afar off, O LORD?<br>_Why_ hidest thou _thyself_ in times of trouble?<br>
<sup>2</sup> The wicked in _his_ pride doth persecute the poor:<sup>a</sup><br>Let them be taken in the devices that they have imagined.<br>

#### (מ Mem)
<sup>3</sup> For the wicked boasteth of his heart’s desire,<br>And blesseth<sup>b</sup> the covetous, _whom_ the LORD abhorreth.<br>

#### (נ Nun)
<sup>4</sup> The wicked, through the pride of his countenance, will not seek _after God:_<br>God _is_ not in all his thoughts.<br>
<sup>5</sup> His ways are always grievous; Thy judgments _are_ far above out of his sight:<br>_As for_ all his enemies, he puffeth at them.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He hath said in his heart, I shall not be moved:<br>For _I shall_ never<sup>c</sup> _be_ in adversity.<br>

#### (ס Samek)
<sup>7</sup> 
#### פ Pe
His mouth is full of cursing and deceit<sup>d</sup> and fraud:<br>Under his tongue _is_ mischief and vanity.<sup>e</sup><br>

#### ע Ain
<sup>8</sup> He sitteth in the lurking places of the villages:<br>In the secret places doth he murder the innocent: His eyes are privily set against the poor.<br>

#### (צ Zade)
<sup>9</sup> He lieth in wait secretly<sup>f</sup> as a lion in his den:<br>He lieth in wait to catch the poor:<br>He doth catch the poor, when he draweth him into his net.<br>
<sup>10</sup> He croucheth,<sup>g</sup> _and_ humbleth himself,<br>That the poor may fall by his strong ones.<sup>h</sup><br>
<sup>11</sup> He hath said in his heart, God hath forgotten:<br>He hideth his face; he will never see _it_.<br>

#### ק Qoph
<sup>12</sup> Arise, O LORD; O God, lift up thine hand:<br>Forget not the humble.<sup>i</sup><br>
<sup>13</sup> Wherefore doth the wicked contemn God?<br>He hath said in his heart, Thou wilt not require _it_.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>14</sup> Thou hast seen _it_; for thou beholdest mischief and spite, to requite _it_ with thy hand:<br>The poor committeth<sup>j</sup> himself unto thee; Thou art the helper of the fatherless.<br>

#### ש Shin
<sup>15</sup> Break thou the arm of the wicked<br>And the evil _man:_ seek out his wickedness _till_ thou find none.<br>
<sup>16</sup> The LORD _is_ King for ever and ever:<br>The heathen are perished out of his land.<br>

#### ת Taw
<sup>17</sup> LORD, thou hast heard the desire of the humble:<br>Thou wilt prepare<sup>k</sup> their heart, thou wilt cause thine ear to hear:<br>
<sup>18</sup> To judge the fatherless and the oppress<sup>l</sup>ed,<br>That the man of the earth may no more oppress.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _in the pride of the wicked he doth persecute_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Or, _the coveteous blesseth himself, he abborreth the Lord_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Heb. _unto generation and generation_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Heb. _deceits_
> - <sup>e</sup> 7. Or, _iniquity_
> - <sup>f</sup> 9. Heb. _in the secret places_
> - <sup>g</sup> 10. Heb. _he breaketh himself_
> - <sup>h</sup> 10. Or, _into his strong parts_
> - <sup>i</sup> 12. Or, _afflicted_
> - <sup>j</sup> 14. Heb. _leaveth_
> - <sup>k</sup> 17. Or, _establish_
> - <sup>l</sup> 18. Or, _terrify_

## Chapter 11

> _To the chief Musician, _A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> In the LORD put I my trust:<br>How say ye to my soul, flee _as_ a bird to your mountain?<br>
<sup>2</sup> For, lo, the wicked bend _their_ bow, they make ready their arrow upon the string,<br>That they may privily<sup>a</sup> shoot at the upright in heart.<br>
<sup>3</sup> If the foundations be destroyed,<br>What can the righteous do?<br>
<sup>4</sup> The LORD _is_ in his holy temple,<br>The LORD’s throne _is_ in heaven:<br>His eyes behold, his eyelids try, the children of men.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The LORD trieth the righteous:<br>But the wicked and him that loveth violence his soul hateth.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Upon the wicked he shall rain snares, fire and brimstone, and an horrible tempest:<sup>b</sup><br>_This shall be_ the portion of their cup.<br>
<sup>7</sup> For the righteous LORD loveth righteousness;<br>His countenance doth behold the upright.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _in darkness_
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Or, _a burning tempest_

## Chapter 12

> _To the chief Musician upon Sheminith, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Help,<sup>a</sup> LORD; for the godly man ceaseth;<br>For the faithful fail from among the children of men.<br>
<sup>2</sup> They speak vanity every one with his neighbour:<br>_With_ flattering lips _and_ with a double heart<sup>b</sup> do they speak.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The LORD shall cut off all flattering lips,<br>_And_ the tongue that speaketh proud things:<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>4</sup> Who have said, With our tongue will we prevail;<br>Our lips _are_ our own:<sup>d</sup> who _is_ lord over us?<br>
<sup>5</sup> For the oppression of the poor,<br>For the sighing of the needy,<br>Now will I arise, saith the LORD;<br>I will set _him_ in safety _from him that_ puffeth at him.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>6</sup> The words of the LORD _are_ pure words:<br>_As_ silver tried in a furnace of earth, purified seven times.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Thou shalt keep them,<sup>f</sup> O LORD,<br>Thou shalt preserve them from this generation for ever.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The wicked walk on every side,<br>When the vilest men are exalted.<sup>g</sup><br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _save_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Heb. _an heart, and an heart_
> - <sup>c</sup> 3. Heb. _great things_
> - <sup>d</sup> 4. Heb. _are with us_
> - <sup>e</sup> 5. Or, _would ensnare him_
> - <sup>f</sup> 7. Heb. _him. i. every one of them_
> - <sup>g</sup> 8. Heb. _the vilest of the sons of the men are exalted_

## Chapter 13

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> How long wilt thou forget me, O LORD? for ever?<br>How long wilt thou hide thy face from me?<br>
<sup>2</sup> How long shall I take counsel in my soul, _Having_ sorrow in my heart daily?<br>How long shall mine enemy be exalted over me?<br>
<sup>3</sup> Consider _and_ hear me, O LORD my God:<br>Lighten mine eyes, lest I sleep the _sleep of_ death;<br>
<sup>4</sup> Lest mine enemy say, I have prevailed against him;<br>_And_ those that trouble me rejoice when I am moved.<br>
<sup>5</sup> But I have trusted in thy mercy;<br>My heart shall rejoice in thy salvation.<br>
<sup>6</sup> I will sing unto the LORD, because he hath dealt bountifully with me.<br>

## Chapter 14

> _To the chief Musician, _A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> The fool hath said in his heart, _there is_ no God.<br>They are corrupt, they have done abominable works,<br>_There is_ none that doeth good.<br>
<sup>2</sup> The LORD looked down from heaven upon the children of men,<br>To see if there were any that did understand, _And_ seek God.<br>
<sup>3</sup> They are all gone aside, they are _all_ together become filthy:<sup>a</sup><br>_There is_ none that doeth good, no, not one.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Have all the workers of iniquity no knowledge?<br>Who eat up my people _as_ they eat bread, and call not upon the LORD.<br>
<sup>5</sup> There were they in great fear:<sup>b</sup><br>For God _is_ in the generation of the righteous.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Ye have shamed the counsel of the poor,<br>Because the LORD _is_ his refuge.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Oh that the salvation of Israel _were come_ out of Zion!<br>When the LORD bringeth back the captivity of his people,<br>Jacob shall rejoice, _and_ Israel shall be glad.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _stinking_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _they feared a fear_

## Chapter 15

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> LORD, who shall abide<sup>a</sup> in thy tabernacle?<br>Who shall dwell in thy holy hill?<br>
<sup>2</sup> He that walketh uprightly,<br>and worketh righteousness, and speaketh the truth in his heart.<br>
<sup>3</sup> _He that_ backbiteth not with his tongue, nor doeth evil to his neighbour,<br>Nor taketh up<sup>b</sup> a reproach against his neighbour.<br>
<sup>4</sup> In whose eyes a vile person is contemned;<br>But he honoureth them that fear the LORD.<br>_He that_ sweareth to _his own_ hurt, and changeth not.<br>
<sup>5</sup> _He that_ putteth not out his money to usury,<br>Nor taketh reward against the innocent.<br>He that doeth these _things_ shall never be moved.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _sojourn_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Or, _receiveth_, or _endureth_

## Chapter 16

> _Michtam of David._

<sup>1</sup> Preserve me, O God:<br>For in thee do I put my trust.<br>
<sup>2</sup> _O my soul_, thou hast said unto the LORD,<br>Thou _art_ my Lord: My goodness _extendeth_ not to thee;<br>
<sup>3</sup> _But_ to the saints that _are_ in the earth,<br>And _to_ the excellent, in whom _is_ all my delight.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Their sorrows shall be multiplied _that_ hasten _after_ another<sup>a</sup> _god:_<br>Their drink offerings of blood will I not offer,<br>Nor take up their names into my lips.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The LORD _is_ the portion of mine<sup>b</sup> inheritance and of my cup:<br>Thou maintainest my lot.<br>
<sup>6</sup> The lines are fallen unto me in pleasant _places_;<br>Yea, I have a goodly heritage.<br>
<sup>7</sup> I will bless the LORD, who hath given me counsel:<br>My reins also instruct me in the night seasons.<br>
<sup>8</sup> I have set the LORD always before me:<br>Because _he is_ at my right hand, I shall not be moved.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Therefore my heart is glad, and my glory rejoiceth:<br>My flesh also shall rest in hope.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>10</sup> For thou wilt not leave my soul in hell;<br>Neither wilt thou suffer thine Holy One to see corruption.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Thou wilt shew me the path of life: In thy presence _is_ fulness of joy;<br>At thy right hand _there are_ pleasures for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Or, _give gifts to another_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _of my part_
> - <sup>c</sup> 9. Heb. _dwell confidently_

## Chapter 17

> _A Prayer of David._

<sup>1</sup> Hear the right,<sup>a</sup> O LORD, attend unto my cry,<br>Give ear unto my prayer, _that goeth_ not out of feigned lips.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let my sentence come forth from thy presence;<br>Let thine eyes behold the things that are equal.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thou hast proved mine heart; thou hast visited _me_ in the night; Thou hast tried me, _and_ shalt find nothing;<br>I am purposed _that_ my mouth shall not transgress.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Concerning the works of men, by the word of thy lips<br>I have kept _me from_ the paths of the destroyer.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Hold up my goings in thy paths, _that_ my footsteps slip not.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>6</sup> I have called upon thee, for thou wilt hear me, O God:<br>Incline thine ear unto me, _and_ hear my speech.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Shew thy marvellous lovingkindness, O thou that savest by thy right hand them which put their trust _in thee_<br>From those that rise up _against them_.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Keep me as the apple of the eye,<br>Hide me under the shadow of thy wings,<br>
<sup>9</sup> From the wicked that oppress me,<sup>c</sup><br>_From_ my deadly enemies,<sup>d</sup> _who_ compass me about.<br>
<sup>10</sup> They are inclosed in their own fat:<br>With their mouth they speak proudly.<br>
<sup>11</sup> They have now compassed us in our steps:<br>They have set their eyes bowing down to the earth;<br>
<sup>12</sup> Like as a lion<sup>e</sup> _that_ is greedy of his prey,<br>And as it were a young lion lurking<sup>f</sup> in secret places.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Arise, O LORD, disappoint him,<sup>g</sup> cast him down:<br>Deliver my soul from the wicked, _which is_ thy sword:<sup>h</sup><br>
<sup>14</sup> From men _which are_ thy hand,<sup>i</sup> O LORD, from men of the world,<br>_Which have_ their portion in _this_ life, and whose belly thou fillest with thy hid _treasure:_<br>They are full of children,<sup>j</sup><br>And leave the rest of their _substance_ to their babes.<br>
<sup>15</sup> As for me, I will behold thy face in righteousness:<br>I shall be satisfied, when I awake, with thy likeness.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _justice_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _be not moved_
> - <sup>c</sup> 9. Heb. _that waste me_
> - <sup>d</sup> 9. Heb. _my enemies against the soul_
> - <sup>e</sup> 12. Heb. _The likeness of him (i. of every one of them) is as a lion that desireth to ravin_
> - <sup>f</sup> 12. Heb. _sitting_
> - <sup>g</sup> 13. Heb. _prevent his face_
> - <sup>h</sup> 13. Or, _by thy sword_
> - <sup>i</sup> 14. Or, _from men by thine hand_
> - <sup>j</sup> 14. Or, _their children are full_

## Chapter 18

> _To the chief Musician, _A Psalm_ of David, the servant of the LORD, who spake unto the LORD the words of this song in the day _that_ the LORD delivered him from the hand of all his enemies, and from the hand of Saul: And he said,_

<sup>1</sup> I will love thee, O LORD, my strength.<br>
<sup>2</sup> The LORD _is_ my rock, and my fortress, and my deliverer;<br>My God, my strength,<sup>a</sup> in whom I will trust;<br>My buckler, and the horn of my salvation, _and_ my high tower.<br>
<sup>3</sup> I will call upon the LORD, _who is worthy_ to be praised:<br>So shall I be saved from mine enemies.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The sorrows of death compassed me,<br>And the floods of ungodly men<sup>b</sup> made me afraid.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The sorrows<sup>c</sup> of hell compassed me about:<br>The snares of death prevented me.<br>
<sup>6</sup> In my distress I called upon the LORD,<br>And cried unto my God:<br>He heard my voice out of his temple,<br>And my cry came before him, _even_ into his ears.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Then the earth shook and trembled;<br>The foundations also of the hills moved and were shaken, because he was wroth.<br>
<sup>8</sup> There went up a smoke out of his<sup>d</sup> nostrils,<br>And fire out of his mouth devoured: Coals were kindled by it.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He bowed the heavens also, and came down:<br>And darkness _was_ under his feet.<br>
<sup>10</sup> And he rode upon a cherub, and did fly:<br>Yea, he did fly upon the wings of the wind.<br>
<sup>11</sup> He made darkness his secret place; his pavilion round about him<br>_Were_ dark waters _and_ thick clouds of the skies.<br>
<sup>12</sup> At the brightness _that was_ before him his thick clouds passed,<br>Hail _stones_ and coals of fire.<br>
<sup>13</sup> The LORD also thundered in the heavens, and the Highest gave his voice;<br>Hail _stones_ and coals of fire.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Yea, he sent out his arrows, and scattered them;<br>And he shot out lightnings, and discomfited them.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Then the channels of waters were seen, and the foundations of the world were discovered at thy rebuke, O LORD,<br>At the blast of the breath of thy nostrils.<br>
<sup>16</sup> He sent from above, he took me,<br>He drew me out of many waters.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>17</sup> He delivered me from my strong enemy, and from them which hated me:<br>For they were too strong for me.<br>
<sup>18</sup> They prevented me in the day of my calamity:<br>But the LORD was my stay.<br>
<sup>19</sup> He brought me forth also into a large place;<br>He delivered me, because he delighted in me.<br>
<sup>20</sup> The LORD rewarded me according to my righteousness;<br>According to the cleanness of my hands hath he recompensed me.<br>
<sup>21</sup> For I have kept the ways of the LORD,<br>And have not wickedly departed from my God.<br>
<sup>22</sup> For all his judgments _were_ before me,<br>And I did not put away his statutes from me.<br>
<sup>23</sup> I was also upright before him,<sup>f</sup><br>And I kept myself from mine iniquity.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Therefore hath the LORD recompensed me according to my righteousness,<br>According to the cleanness of my hands in his eyesight.<sup>g</sup><br>
<sup>25</sup> With the merciful thou wilt shew thyself merciful;<br>With an upright man thou wilt shew thyself upright;<br>
<sup>26</sup> With the pure thou wilt shew thyself pure;<br>And with the froward thou wilt shew thyself froward.<sup>h</sup><br>
<sup>27</sup> For thou wilt save the afflicted people;<br>But wilt bring down high looks.<br>
<sup>28</sup> For thou wilt light my candle:<sup>i</sup><br>The LORD my God will enlighten my darkness.<br>
<sup>29</sup> For by thee I have run through<sup>j</sup> a troop;<br>And by my God have I leaped over a wall.<br>
<sup>30</sup> _As for_ God, his way _is_ perfect:<br>The word of the LORD is tried:<sup>k</sup> He _is_ a buckler to all those that trust in him.<br>
<sup>31</sup> For who _is_ God save the LORD?<br>Or who _is_ a rock save our God?<br>
<sup>32</sup> _It is_ God that girdeth me with strength,<br>And maketh my way perfect.<br>
<sup>33</sup> He maketh my feet like hinds’ _feet_,<br>And setteth me upon my high places.<br>
<sup>34</sup> He teacheth my hands to war,<br>So that a bow of steel is broken by mine arms.<br>
<sup>35</sup> Thou hast also given me the shield of thy salvation:<br>And thy right hand hath holden me up, and thy gentleness hath made me great.<sup>l</sup><br>
<sup>36</sup> Thou hast enlarged my steps under me,<br>That my feet<sup>m</sup> did not slip.<br>
<sup>37</sup> I have pursued mine enemies, and overtaken them:<br>Neither did I turn again till they were consumed.<br>
<sup>38</sup> I have wounded them that they were not able to rise:<br>They are fallen under my feet.<br>
<sup>39</sup> For thou hast girded me with strength unto the battle:<br>Thou hast subdued<sup>n</sup> under me those that rose up against me.<br>
<sup>40</sup> Thou hast also given me the necks of mine enemies;<br>That I might destroy them that hate me.<br>
<sup>41</sup> They cried, but _there was_ none to save _them:_<br>_Even_ unto the LORD, but he answered them not.<br>
<sup>42</sup> Then did I beat them small as the dust before the wind:<br>I did cast them out as the dirt in the streets.<br>
<sup>43</sup> Thou hast delivered me from the strivings of the people;<br>_And_ thou hast made me the head of the heathen:<br>A people _whom_<br>I have not known shall serve me.<br>
<sup>44</sup> As soon as they hear of me, they shall obey me:<br>The strangers<sup>o</sup> shall submit<sup>p</sup> themselves unto me.<br>
<sup>45</sup> The strangers shall fade away,<br>And be afraid out of their close places.<br>
<sup>46</sup> The LORD liveth; and blessed _be_ my rock;<br>And let the God of my salvation be exalted.<br>
<sup>47</sup> _It is_ God that avengeth<sup>q</sup> me,<br>And subdueth<sup>r</sup> the people under me.<br>
<sup>48</sup> He delivereth me from mine enemies: Yea, thou liftest me up above those that rise up against me:<br>Thou hast delivered me from the violent man.<sup>s</sup><br>
<sup>49</sup> Therefore will I give<sup>t</sup> thanks unto thee, O LORD, among the heathen,<br>And sing praises unto thy name.<br>
<sup>50</sup> Great deliverance giveth he to his king;<br>And sheweth mercy to his anointed, to David, and to his seed for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _my rock_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Heb. _Belial_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Or, _cords_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Heb. _by his_
> - <sup>e</sup> 16. Or, _great waters_
> - <sup>f</sup> 23. Heb. _with_
> - <sup>g</sup> 24. Heb. _before his eyes_
> - <sup>h</sup> 26. Or, _wrestle_
> - <sup>i</sup> 28. Or, _lamp_
> - <sup>j</sup> 29. Or, _broken_
> - <sup>k</sup> 30. Or, _refined_
> - <sup>l</sup> 35. Or, _with thy meekness thou hast multiplied me_
> - <sup>m</sup> 36. Heb. _mine ankles_
> - <sup>n</sup> 39. Heb. _caused to bow_
> - <sup>o</sup> 44. Heb. _the sons of the stranger_
> - <sup>p</sup> 44. Or, _yield feigned obedience_
> - <sup>q</sup> 47. Heb. _giveth avengements for me_
> - <sup>r</sup> 47. Or, _destroyeth_
> - <sup>s</sup> 48. Heb. _man of violence_
> - <sup>t</sup> 49. Or, _confess_

## Chapter 19

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> The heavens declare the glory of God;<br>And the firmament sheweth his handywork.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Day unto day uttereth speech,<br>And night unto night sheweth knowledge.
<sup>3</sup> _There is_ no speech nor language,<br>_Where_ their voice is not heard.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>4</sup> Their line<sup>b</sup> is gone out through all the earth,<br>And their words to the end of the world.<br>In them hath he set a tabernacle for the sun,<br>
<sup>5</sup> Which _is_ as a bridegroom coming out of his chamber, _and_ rejoiceth as a strong man to run a race.<br>
<sup>6</sup> His going forth _is_ from the end of the heaven, and his circuit unto the ends of it:<br>And there is nothing hid from the heat thereof.<br>
<sup>7</sup> The law<sup>c</sup> of the LORD _is_ perfect, converting<sup>d</sup> the soul:<br>The testimony of the LORD _is_ sure, making wise the simple.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The statutes of the LORD _are_ right, rejoicing the heart:<br>The commandment of the LORD _is_ pure, enlightening the eyes.<br>
<sup>9</sup> The fear of the LORD _is_ clean, enduring for ever:<br>The judgments of the LORD _are_ true<sup>e</sup> _and_ righteous altogether.<br>
<sup>10</sup> More to be desired _are they_ than gold, yea, than much fine gold:<br>Sweeter also than honey and the honeycomb.<sup>f</sup><br>
<sup>11</sup> Moreover by them is thy servant warned:<br>_And_ in keeping of them _there is_ great reward.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Who can understand _his_ errors?<br>Cleanse thou me from secret _faults_.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Keep back thy servant also from presumptuous _sins_; Let them not have dominion over me:<br>Then shall I be upright, and I shall be innocent from the great<sup>g</sup> transgression.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Let the words of my mouth, and the meditation of my heart, be acceptable in thy sight,<br>O LORD, my strength,<sup>h</sup> and my redeemer.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _without these their voice is heard_. Heb. _without their voice heard_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Or, _their rule_, or _direction_
> - <sup>c</sup> 7. Or, _doctrine_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Or, _restoring_
> - <sup>e</sup> 9. Heb. _truth_
> - <sup>f</sup> 10. Heb. _the dropping of honeycombs_
> - <sup>g</sup> 13. Or, _much_
> - <sup>h</sup> 14. Heb. _my rock_

## Chapter 20

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> The LORD hear thee in the day of trouble;<br>The name of the God of Jacob defend thee;<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>2</sup> Send thee help<sup>b</sup> from the sanctuary,<br>And strengthen thee<sup>c</sup> out of Zion;<br>
<sup>3</sup> Remember all thy offerings,<br>And accept<sup>d</sup> thy burnt sacrifice; _Selah._<br>
<sup>4</sup> Grant thee according to thine own heart,<br>And fulfil all thy counsel.<br>
<sup>5</sup> We will rejoice in thy salvation, and in the name of our God we will set up _our_ banners:<br>The LORD fulfil all thy petitions.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Now know I that the LORD saveth his anointed; He will hear him from his holy heaven<sup>e</sup><br>With the saving strength of his right hand.<sup>f</sup><br>
<sup>7</sup> Some _trust_ in chariots, and some in horses:<br>But we will remember the name of the LORD our God.<br>
<sup>8</sup> They are brought down and fallen:<br>But we are risen, and stand upright.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Save, LORD:<br>Let the king hear us when we call.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _set thee on an high place_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Heb. _thy help_
> - <sup>c</sup> 2. Heb. _support thee_
> - <sup>d</sup> 3. Heb. _turn to ashes_, or, _make fat_
> - <sup>e</sup> 6. Heb. _from the heaven of his holiness_
> - <sup>f</sup> 6. Heb. _by the strength of the salvation of his right hand_

## Chapter 21

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> The king shall joy in thy strength, O LORD;<br>And in thy salvation how greatly shall he rejoice!<br>
<sup>2</sup> Thou hast given him his heart’s desire,<br>And hast not withholden the request of his lips. _Selah._<br>
<sup>3</sup> For thou preventest him with the blessings of goodness:<br>Thou settest a crown of pure gold on his head.<br>
<sup>4</sup> He asked life of thee, _and_ thou gavest _it_ him,<br>_Even_ length of days for ever and ever.<br>
<sup>5</sup> His glory _is_ great in thy salvation:<br>Honour and majesty hast thou laid upon him.<br>
<sup>6</sup> For thou hast made him most blessed<sup>a</sup> for ever:<br>Thou hast made him exceeding glad<sup>b</sup> with thy countenance.<br>
<sup>7</sup> For the king trusteth in the LORD,<br>And through the mercy of the Most High he shall not be moved.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Thine hand shall find out all thine enemies:<br>Thy right hand shall find out those that hate thee.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Thou shalt make them as a fiery oven in the time of thine anger:<br>The LORD shall swallow them up in his wrath, and the fire shall devour them.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Their fruit shalt thou destroy from the earth,<br>And their seed from among the children of men.<br>
<sup>11</sup> For they intended evil against thee:<br>They imagined a mischievous device, _which_ they are not able _to perform_.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Therefore shalt thou make them turn<sup>c</sup> their back,<sup>d</sup><br>_When_ thou shalt make ready _thine arrows_ upon thy strings against the face of them.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Be thou exalted, LORD, in thine own strength:<br>_So_ will we sing and praise thy power.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Heb. _set him to be blessings_
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Heb. _gladded him with joy_
> - <sup>c</sup> 12. Or, _thou shalt set them as a butte_
> - <sup>d</sup> 12. Heb. _shoulder_

## Chapter 22

> _To the chief Musician upon Aijeleth Shahar, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> My God, my God, why hast thou forsaken me?<br>_Why art thou so_ far from helping me,<sup>a</sup> _and from_ the words of my roaring?<br>
<sup>2</sup> O my God, I cry in the daytime, but thou hearest not;<br>And in the night season, and am not silent.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> But thou _art_ holy,<br>_O thou_ that inhabitest the praises of Israel.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Our fathers trusted in thee:<br>They trusted, and thou didst deliver them.<br>
<sup>5</sup> They cried unto thee, and were delivered:<br>They trusted in thee, and were not confounded.<br>
<sup>6</sup> But I _am_ a worm, and no man;<br>A reproach of men, and despised of the people.<br>
<sup>7</sup> All they that see me laugh me to scorn:<br>They shoot<sup>c</sup> out the lip, they shake the head, _saying_,<br>
<sup>8</sup> He trusted on the LORD<sup>d</sup> _that_ he would deliver him:<br>Let him deliver him, seeing he delighted in him.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>9</sup> But thou _art_ he that took me out of the womb:<br>Thou didst make me hope<sup>f</sup> _when I was_ upon my mother’s breasts.<br>
<sup>10</sup> I was cast upon thee from the womb:<br>Thou _art_ my God from my mother’s belly.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Be not far from me; for trouble _is_ near;<br>For _there is_ none to help.<sup>g</sup><br>
<sup>12</sup> Many bulls have compassed me:<br>Strong _bulls_ of Bashan have beset me round.<br>
<sup>13</sup> They gaped upon me _with_ their mouths,<sup>h</sup><br>_As_ a ravening and a roaring lion.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I am poured out like water, and all my bones are out of joint:<sup>i</sup><br>My heart is like wax; It is melted in the midst of my bowels.<br>
<sup>15</sup> My strength is dried up like a potsherd; And my tongue cleaveth to my jaws;<br>And thou hast brought me into the dust of death.<br>
<sup>16</sup> For dogs have compassed me:<br>The assembly of the wicked have inclosed me:<br>They pierced my hands and my feet.
<sup>17</sup> I may tell all my bones:<br>They look _and_ stare upon me.<br>
<sup>18</sup> They part my garments among them,<br>And cast lots upon my vesture.<br>
<sup>19</sup> But be not thou far from me, O LORD:<br>O my strength, haste thee to help me.<br>
<sup>20</sup> Deliver my soul from the sword;<br>My darling<sup>j</sup> from the power<sup>k</sup> of the dog.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Save me from the lion’s mouth:<br>For thou hast heard me from the horns of the unicorns.<br>
<sup>22</sup> I will declare thy name unto my brethren:<br>In the midst of the congregation will I praise thee.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Ye that fear the LORD, praise him;<br>All ye the seed of Jacob, glorify him; And fear him, all ye the seed of Israel.<br>
<sup>24</sup> For he hath not despised nor abhorred the affliction of the afflicted;<br>Neither hath he hid his face from him; But when he cried unto him, he heard.<br>
<sup>25</sup> My praise _shall be_ of thee in the great congregation:<br>I will pay my vows before them that fear him.<br>
<sup>26</sup> The meek shall eat and be satisfied:<br>They shall praise the LORD that seek him: Your heart shall live for ever.<br>
<sup>27</sup> All the ends of the world shall remember and turn unto the LORD:<br>And all the kindreds of the nations shall worship before thee.<br>
<sup>28</sup> For the kingdom _is_ the LORD’s:<br>And he _is_ the governor among the nations.<br>
<sup>29</sup> All _they that be_ fat upon earth shall eat and worship:<br>All they that go down to the dust shall bow before him:<br>And none can keep alive his own soul.<br>
<sup>30</sup> A seed shall serve him;<br>It shall be accounted to the LORD for a generation.<br>
<sup>31</sup> They shall come, and shall declare his righteousness<br>Unto a people that shall be born, that he hath done _this_.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _from my salvation_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Heb. _there is no silence to me_
> - <sup>c</sup> 7. Heb. _open_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Heb. _He rolled himself on the Lord_
> - <sup>e</sup> 8. Or, _if he delight in him_
> - <sup>f</sup> 9. Or, _keptest me in safety_
> - <sup>g</sup> 11. Heb. _not a helper_
> - <sup>h</sup> 13. Heb. _opened their mouths against me_
> - <sup>i</sup> 14. Or, _sundry_
> - <sup>j</sup> 20. Heb. _my only one_
> - <sup>k</sup> 20. Heb. _from the hand_

## Chapter 23

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> The LORD _is_ my shepherd; I shall not want.<br>
<sup>2</sup> He maketh me to lie down in green pastures:<sup>a</sup><br>He leadeth me beside the still waters.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> He restoreth my soul:<br>He leadeth me in the paths of righteousness for his name’s sake.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Yea, though I walk through the valley of the shadow of death, I will fear no evil:<br>for thou _art_ with me; Thy rod and thy staff they comfort me.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Thou preparest a table before me in the presence of mine enemies:<br>Thou anointest<sup>c</sup> my head with oil; my cup runneth over.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Surely goodness and mercy shall follow me all the days of my life:<br>And I will dwell in the house of the LORD for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _pastures of tender grass_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. _waters of quietness_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Heb. _makest fat_

## Chapter 24

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> The earth _is_ the LORD’s, and the fulness thereof;<br>The world, and they that dwell therein.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For he hath founded it upon the seas,<br>And established it upon the floods.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Who shall ascend into the hill of the LORD?<br>Or who shall stand in his holy place?<br>
<sup>4</sup> He that hath clean hands,<sup>a</sup> and a pure heart;<br>Who hath not lifted up his soul unto vanity, nor sworn deceitfully.<br>
<sup>5</sup> He shall receive the blessing from the LORD,<br>And righteousness from the God of his salvation.<br>
<sup>6</sup> This _is_ the generation of them that seek him,<br>That seek thy face, O Jacob.<sup>b</sup> _Selah._<br>
<sup>7</sup> Lift up your heads, O ye gates;<br>And be ye lift up, ye everlasting doors; And the King of glory shall come in.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Who _is_ this King of glory?<br>The LORD strong and mighty, the LORD mighty in battle.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Lift up your heads, O ye gates; even lift _them_ up, ye everlasting doors;<br>And the King of glory shall come in.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Who is this King of glory?<br>The LORD of hosts, he _is_ the King of glory. _Selah._<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Heb. _the clean of hands_
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Or, _O God of Jacob_

## Chapter 25

> __A Psalm_ of David._

#### א Aleph

<sup>1</sup> Unto thee, O LORD, do I lift up my soul.

#### ב Bet
<sup>2</sup> O my God, I trust in thee:<br>Let me not be ashamed, let not mine enemies triumph over me.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>3</sup> Yea, let none that wait on thee be ashamed:<br>Let them be ashamed which transgress without cause.<br>

#### ד Dalet
<sup>4</sup> Shew me thy ways, O LORD;<br>Teach me thy paths.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>5</sup> 
#### (ו Waw)
Lead me in thy truth, and teach me:<br>For thou _art_ the God of my salvation; on thee do I wait all the day.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>6</sup> Remember, O LORD, thy tender mercies and thy lovingkindnesses;<br>For they _have been_ ever of old.<br>

#### ח Het
<sup>7</sup> Remember not the sins of my youth, nor my transgressions:<br>According to thy mercy remember thou me for thy goodness’ sake, O LORD.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>8</sup> Good and upright _is_ the LORD:<br>Therefore will he teach sinners in the way.<br>

#### י Yod
<sup>9</sup> The meek will he guide in judgment:<br>And the meek will he teach his way.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>10</sup> All the paths of the LORD _are_ mercy and truth<br>Unto such as keep his covenant and his testimonies.<br>

#### ל Lamed
<sup>11</sup> For thy name’s sake, O LORD,<br>Pardon mine iniquity; for it _is_ great.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>12</sup> What man _is_ he that feareth the LORD?<br>Him shall he teach in the way _that_ he shall choose.<br>

#### נ Nun
<sup>13</sup> His soul shall dwell at ease;<br>And his seed shall inherit the earth.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>14</sup> The secret of the LORD _is_ with them that fear him;<br>And he will shew them his covenant.<br>

#### ע Ain
<sup>15</sup> Mine eyes _are_ ever toward the LORD;<br>For he shall pluck<sup>a</sup> my feet out of the net.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>16</sup> Turn thee unto me, and have mercy upon me;<br>For I _am_ desolate and afflicted.<br>

#### צ Zade
<sup>17</sup> The troubles of my heart are enlarged:<br>_O_ bring thou me out of my distresses.<br>

#### (ק Qoph)
<sup>18</sup> Look upon mine affliction and my pain;<br>And forgive all my sins.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>19</sup> Consider mine enemies; for they are many;<br>And they hate me with cruel hatred.<sup>b</sup><br>

#### ש Shin
<sup>20</sup> O keep my soul, and deliver me:<br>Let me not be ashamed; for I put my trust in thee.<br>

#### ת Taw
<sup>21</sup> Let integrity and uprightness preserve me;<br>For I wait on thee.<br>
<sup>22</sup> Redeem Israel, O God, out of all his troubles.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 15. Heb. _bring forth_
> - <sup>b</sup> 19. Heb. _hatred of violence_

## Chapter 26

> __A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> Judge me, O LORD; for I have walked in mine integrity:<br>I have trusted also in the LORD; _therefore_ I shall not slide.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Examine me, O LORD, and prove me;<br>Try my reins and my heart.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For thy lovingkindness _is_ before mine eyes:<br>And I have walked in thy truth.<br>
<sup>4</sup> I have not sat with vain persons,<br>Neither will I go in with dissemblers.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I have hated the congregation of evil doers;<br>And will not sit with the wicked.<br>
<sup>6</sup> I will wash mine hands in innocency:<br>So will I compass thine altar, O LORD:<br>
<sup>7</sup> That I may publish with the voice of thanksgiving,<br>And tell of all thy wondrous works.<br>
<sup>8</sup> LORD, I have loved the habitation of thy house,<br>And the place where thine honour dwelleth.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Gather not<sup>a</sup> my soul with sinners,<br>Nor my life with bloody men:<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>10</sup> In whose hands _is_ mischief,<br>And their right hand is full of bribes.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>11</sup> But as for me, I will walk in mine integrity:<br>Redeem me, and be merciful unto me.<br>
<sup>12</sup> My foot standeth in an even place:<br>In the congregations will I bless the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 9. Or, _take not away_
> - <sup>b</sup> 9. Heb. _men of blood_
> - <sup>c</sup> 10. Heb. _filled with_

## Chapter 27

> __A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> The LORD _is_ my light and my salvation; whom shall I fear?<br>The LORD _is_ the strength of my life; of whom shall I be afraid?<br>
<sup>2</sup> When the wicked, _even_ mine enemies and my foes, came upon me<sup>a</sup> to eat up my flesh,<br>They stumbled and fell.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Though an host should encamp against me, my heart shall not fear:<br>Though war should rise against me, in this _will_ I _be_ confident.<br>
<sup>4</sup> One _thing_ have I desired of the LORD, that will I seek after;<br>That I may dwell in the house of the LORD all the days of my life,<br>To behold the beauty<sup>b</sup> of the LORD, and to enquire in his temple.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For in the time of trouble he shall hide me in his pavilion:<br>In the secret of his tabernacle shall he hide me; he shall set me up upon a rock.<br>
<sup>6</sup> And now shall mine head be lifted up,<br>Above mine enemies round about me:<br>Therefore will I offer in his tabernacle sacrifices of joy;<sup>c</sup><br>I will sing, yea, I will sing praises unto the LORD.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Hear, O LORD, _when_ I cry with my voice:<br>Have mercy also upon me, and answer me.<br>
<sup>8</sup> _When thou saidst_, Seek ye my face; my heart said unto thee,<sup>d</sup><br>Thy face, LORD, will I seek.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Hide not thy face _far_ from me;<br>Put not thy servant away in anger:<br>Thou hast been my help;<br>Leave me not, neither forsake me, O God of my salvation.<br>
<sup>10</sup> When my father and my mother forsake me,<br>Then the LORD will take me up.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>11</sup> Teach me thy way, O LORD,<br>And lead me in a plain path,<sup>f</sup> because of mine enemies.<sup>g</sup><br>
<sup>12</sup> Deliver me not over unto the will of mine enemies:<br>For false witnesses are risen up against me, and such as breathe out cruelty.<br>
<sup>13</sup> _I had fainted_,<br>Unless I had believed to see the goodness of the LORD in the land of the living.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Wait on the LORD:<br>Be of good courage, and he shall strengthen thine heart: wait, I say, on the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _approached against me_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Or, _the delight_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Heb. _of shouting_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Or, _my heart said unto thee, let my face seek thy face_, etc.
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Heb. _will gather me_
> - <sup>f</sup> 11. Heb. _a way of plainness_
> - <sup>g</sup> 11. Heb. _those which observe me_

## Chapter 28

> __A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> Unto thee will I cry, O LORD my rock; be not silent to me:<sup>a</sup><br>Lest, _if_ thou be silent to me, I become like them that go down into the pit.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Hear the voice of my supplications, when I cry unto thee,<br>When I lift up my hands toward thy holy oracle.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> Draw me not away with the wicked, and with the workers of iniquity,<br>Which speak peace to their neighbours, but mischief _is_ in their hearts.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Give them according to their deeds,<br>And according to the wickedness of their endeavours:<br>Give them after the work of their hands;<br>Render to them their desert.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Because they regard not the works of the LORD, nor the operation of his hands,<br>He shall destroy them, and not build them up.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Blessed _be_ the LORD,<br>Because he hath heard the voice of my supplications.<br>
<sup>7</sup> The LORD _is_ my strength and my shield; my heart trusted in him, and I am helped:<br>Therefore my heart greatly rejoiceth; and with my song will I praise him.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The LORD _is_ their strength,<sup>c</sup><br>And he _is_ the saving strength<sup>d</sup> of his anointed.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Save thy people, and bless thine inheritance:<br>Feed<sup>e</sup> them also, and lift them up for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _from me_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Or, _towards the Oracle of thy Sanctuary_
> - <sup>c</sup> 8. Or, _his strength_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Heb. _strength of salvations_
> - <sup>e</sup> 9. Or, _rule_

## Chapter 29

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Give unto the LORD, O ye mighty,<sup>a</sup> give unto the LORD glory and strength.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Give unto the LORD the glory due unto his name;<sup>b</sup><br>Worship the LORD in the beauty of holiness.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> The voice of the LORD _is_ upon the waters:<br>The God of glory thundereth:<br>The LORD _is_ upon many waters.<sup>d</sup>
<sup>4</sup> The voice of the LORD _is_ powerful;<sup>e</sup><br>The voice of the LORD _is_ full of majesty.<sup>f</sup><br>
<sup>5</sup> The voice of the LORD breaketh the cedars;<br>Yea, the LORD breaketh the cedars of Lebanon.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He maketh them also to skip like a calf;<br>Lebanon and Sirion like a young unicorn.<br>
<sup>7</sup> The voice of the LORD divideth<sup>g</sup> the flames of fire.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The voice of the LORD shaketh<sup>h</sup> the wilderness; the LORD shaketh the wilderness of Kadesh.<br>
<sup>9</sup> The voice of the LORD maketh the hinds to calve, and discovereth the forests:<br>And in his temple doth every one speak<sup>i</sup> of _his_ glory.<br>
<sup>10</sup> The LORD sitteth upon the flood;<br>Yea, the LORD sitteth King for ever.<br>
<sup>11</sup> The LORD will give strength unto his people;<br>The LORD will bless his people with peace.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _ye sons of the mighty_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Heb. _the honour of his name_
> - <sup>c</sup> 2. Or, _in his glorious Sanctuary_
> - <sup>d</sup> 3. Or, _great waters_
> - <sup>e</sup> 4. Heb. _in power_
> - <sup>f</sup> 4. Heb. _in Majesty_
> - <sup>g</sup> 7. Heb. _cutteth out_
> - <sup>h</sup> 8. Or, _to be in pain_
> - <sup>i</sup> 9. Or, _every whit of it uttereth_ etc.

## Chapter 30

> _A Psalm _and_ Song _at_ the dedication of the house of David._

<sup>1</sup> I will extol thee, O LORD; for thou hast lifted me up,<br>And hast not made my foes to rejoice over me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> O LORD my God,<br>I cried unto thee, and thou hast healed me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> O LORD, thou hast brought up my soul from the grave:<br>Thou hast kept me alive, that I should not go down to the pit.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Sing unto the LORD, O ye saints of his,<br>And give thanks at the remembrance of his holiness.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For his anger _endureth but_ a moment;<sup>a</sup> in his favour _is_ life:<br>Weeping may endure for a night,<sup>b</sup> but joy<sup>c</sup> _cometh_ in the morning.<br>
<sup>6</sup> And in my prosperity<sup>d</sup> I said, I shall never be moved.<br>
<sup>7</sup> LORD, by thy favour thou hast made my mountain to stand strong:<sup>e</sup><br>Thou didst hide thy face, _and_ I was troubled.<br>
<sup>8</sup> I cried to thee, O LORD;<br>And unto the LORD I made supplication.<br>
<sup>9</sup> What profit _is there_ in my blood,<br>When I go down to the pit?<br>Shall the dust praise thee?<br>Shall it declare thy truth?<br>
<sup>10</sup> Hear, O LORD, and have mercy upon me:<br>LORD, be thou my helper.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Thou hast turned for me my mourning into dancing:<br>Thou hast put off my sackcloth, and girded me with gladness;<br>
<sup>12</sup> To the end that _my_ glory<sup>f</sup> may sing praise to thee, and not be silent.<br>O LORD my God, I will give thanks unto thee for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Heb. _there is but a moment in his anger_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _in the evening_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Heb. _singing_
> - <sup>d</sup> 6. _[marginal note missing in original]_
> - <sup>e</sup> 7. Heb. _settled strength_
> - <sup>f</sup> 12. That is, _my tongue_, or _my soul_

## Chapter 31

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> In thee, O LORD, do I put my trust;<br>Let me never be ashamed: deliver me in thy righteousness.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Bow down thine ear to me; deliver me speedily:<br>Be thou my strong rock,<sup>a</sup> for an house of defence to save me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For thou _art_ my rock and my fortress;<br>Therefore for thy name’s sake lead me, and guide me.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Pull me out of the net that they have laid privily for me:<br>For thou _art_ my strength.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Into thine hand I commit my spirit:<br>Thou hast redeemed me, O LORD God of truth.<br>
<sup>6</sup> I have hated them that regard lying vanities:<br>But I trust in the LORD.<br>
<sup>7</sup> I will be glad and rejoice in thy mercy:<br>For thou hast considered my trouble; thou hast known my soul in adversities;<br>
<sup>8</sup> And hast not shut me up into the hand of the enemy:<br>Thou hast set my feet in a large room.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Have mercy upon me, O LORD, for I am in trouble:<br>Mine eye is consumed with grief, _yea_, my soul and my belly.<br>
<sup>10</sup> For my life is spent with grief,<br>And my years with sighing:<br>My strength faileth because of mine iniquity,<br>And my bones are consumed.<br>
<sup>11</sup> I was a reproach among all mine enemies, but especially among my neighbours,<br>And a fear to mine acquaintance: they that did see me without fled from me.<br>
<sup>12</sup> I am forgotten as a dead man out of mind:<br>I am like a broken vessel.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>13</sup> For I have heard the slander of many:<br>Fear _was_ on every side: while they took counsel together against me, They devised to take away my life.<br>
<sup>14</sup> But I trusted in thee, O LORD:<br>I said, Thou _art_ my God.<br>
<sup>15</sup> My times _are_ in thy hand:<br>Deliver me from the hand of mine enemies, and from them that persecute me.<br>
<sup>16</sup> Make thy face to shine upon thy servant:<br>Save me for thy mercies’ sake.<br>
<sup>17</sup> Let me not be ashamed, O LORD; for I have called upon thee:<br>Let the wicked be ashamed, _and_ let them be silent in the grave.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>18</sup> Let the lying lips be put to silence;<br>Which speak grievous<sup>d</sup> things proudly and contemptuously against the righteous.<br>
<sup>19</sup> _Oh_ how great _is_ thy goodness, which thou hast laid up for them that fear thee;<br>_Which_ thou hast wrought for them that trust in thee before the sons of men!<br>
<sup>20</sup> Thou shalt hide them in the secret of thy presence from the pride of man:<br>Thou shalt keep them secretly in a pavilion from the strife of tongues.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Blessed _be_ the LORD:<br>For he hath shewed me his marvellous kindness in a strong city.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>22</sup> For I said in my haste,<br>I am cut off from before thine eyes:<br>Nevertheless thou heardest the voice of<br>My supplications when I cried unto thee.<br>
<sup>23</sup> O love the LORD, all ye his saints:<br>_For_ the LORD preserveth the faithful, and plentifully rewardeth the proud doer.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Be of good courage, and he shall strengthen your heart,<br>All ye that hope in the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _to me for a rock of strength_
> - <sup>b</sup> 12. Heb. _a vessel that perisheth_
> - <sup>c</sup> 17. Or, _let them be cut off for the grave_
> - <sup>d</sup> 18. Heb. _a hard thing_
> - <sup>e</sup> 21. Or, _fenced city_

## Chapter 32

> __A Psalm_ of David, Maschil._

<sup>1</sup> Blessed _is he whose_ transgression _is_ forgiven,<br>_Whose_ sin _is_ covered.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Blessed _is_ the man unto whom the LORD imputeth not iniquity,<br>And in whose spirit _there is_ no guile.<br>
<sup>3</sup> When I kept silence, my bones waxed old<br>Through my roaring all the day long.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For day and night thy hand was heavy upon me:<br>My moisture is turned into the drought of summer. _Selah._<br>
<sup>5</sup> I acknowledged my sin unto thee,<br>And mine iniquity have I not hid.<br>I said, I will confess my transgressions unto the LORD;<br>And thou forgavest the iniquity of my sin. _Selah._<br>
<sup>6</sup> For this shall every one that is godly pray unto thee in a time when thou mayest be found:<sup>a</sup><br>Surely in the floods of great waters they shall not come nigh unto him.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Thou _art_ my hiding place; thou shalt preserve me from trouble;<br>Thou shalt compass me about with songs of deliverance. _Selah._<br>
<sup>8</sup> I will instruct thee and teach thee in the way which thou shalt go:<br>I will guide thee with mine eye.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>9</sup> Be ye not as the horse, _or_ as the mule, _which_ have no understanding:<br>Whose mouth must be held in with bit and bridle, lest they come near unto thee.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Many sorrows _shall be_ to the wicked:<br>But he that trusteth in the LORD, mercy shall compass him about.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Be glad in the LORD, and rejoice, ye righteous:<br>And shout for joy, all _ye that are_ upright in heart.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Heb. _in a time of finding_
> - <sup>b</sup> 8. Heb. _I will counsel thee, mine eye shall be upon thee_

## Chapter 33

<sup>1</sup> Rejoice in the LORD, O ye righteous:<br>_For_ praise is comely for the upright.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Praise the LORD with harp:<br>Sing unto him with the psaltery _and_ an instrument of ten strings.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Sing unto him a new song;<br>Play skilfully with a loud noise.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For the word of the LORD _is_ right;<br>And all his works _are done_ in truth.<br>
<sup>5</sup> He loveth righteousness and judgment:<br>The earth is full of the goodness<sup>a</sup> of the LORD.<br>
<sup>6</sup> By the word of the LORD were the heavens made;<br>And all the host of them by the breath of his mouth.<br>
<sup>7</sup> He gathereth the waters of the sea together as an heap:<br>He layeth up the depth in storehouses.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Let all the earth fear the LORD:<br>Let all the inhabitants of the world stand in awe of him.<br>
<sup>9</sup> For he spake, and it was _done_;<br>He commanded, and it stood fast.<br>
<sup>10</sup> The LORD bringeth<sup>b</sup> the counsel of the heathen to nought:<br>He maketh the devices of the people of none effect.<br>
<sup>11</sup> The counsel of the LORD standeth for ever,<br>The thoughts of his heart to all generations.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>12</sup> Blessed _is_ the nation whose God _is_ the LORD;<br>_And_ the people _whom_ he hath chosen for his own inheritance.<br>
<sup>13</sup> The LORD looketh from heaven; he beholdeth all the sons of men.<br>
<sup>14</sup> From the place of his habitation he looketh upon all the inhabitants of the earth.<br>
<sup>15</sup> He fashioneth their hearts alike;<br>He considereth all their works.<br>
<sup>16</sup> There is no king saved by the multitude of an host:<br>A mighty man is not delivered by much strength.<br>
<sup>17</sup> An horse _is_ a vain thing for safety:<br>Neither shall he deliver _any_ by his great strength.<br>
<sup>18</sup> Behold, the eye of the LORD _is_ upon them that fear him,<br>Upon them that hope in his mercy;<br>
<sup>19</sup> To deliver their soul from death,<br>And to keep them alive in famine.<br>
<sup>20</sup> Our soul waiteth for the LORD:<br>He _is_ our help and our shield.<br>
<sup>21</sup> For our heart shall rejoice in him,<br>Because we have trusted in his holy name.<br>
<sup>22</sup> Let thy mercy, O LORD, be upon us,<br>According as we hope in thee.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Or, _mercy_
> - <sup>b</sup> 10. Heb. _maketh frustrate_
> - <sup>c</sup> 11. Heb. _to generation and generation_

## Chapter 34

> __A Psalm_ of David, when he changed his behaviour before Abimelech; who drove him away, and he departed._

#### א Aleph

<sup>1</sup> I will bless the LORD at all times:<br>His praise _shall_ continually _be_ in my mouth.<br>

#### ב Bet
<sup>2</sup> My soul shall make her boast in the LORD:<br>The humble shall hear _thereof_, and be glad.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>3</sup> O magnify the LORD with me,<br>And let us exalt his name together.<br>

#### ד Dalet
<sup>4</sup> I sought the LORD, and he heard me,<br>And delivered me from all my fears.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>5</sup> They looked unto him,<sup>a</sup> and were lightened:<br>And their faces were not ashamed.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>6</sup> This poor man cried, and the LORD heard _him_,<br>And saved him out of all his troubles.<br>

#### ח Het
<sup>7</sup> The angel of the LORD encampeth round about them that fear him,<br>And delivereth them.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>8</sup> O taste and see that the LORD _is_ good:<br>Blessed _is_ the man _that_ trusteth in him.<br>

#### י Yod
<sup>9</sup> O fear the LORD, ye his saints:<br>For _there is_ no want to them that fear him.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>10</sup> The young lions do lack, and suffer hunger:<br>But they that seek the LORD shall not want any good _thing_.<br>

#### ל Lamed
<sup>11</sup> Come, ye children, hearken unto me:<br>I will teach you the fear of the LORD.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>12</sup> What man _is he that_ desireth life,<br>_And_ loveth _many_ days, that he may see good?<br>

#### נ Nun
<sup>13</sup> Keep thy tongue from evil,<br>And thy lips from speaking guile.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>14</sup> Depart from evil, and do good;<br>Seek peace, and pursue it.<br>

#### ע Ain
<sup>15</sup> The eyes of the LORD _are_ upon the righteous,<br>And his ears _are open_ unto their cry.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>16</sup> The face of the LORD _is_ against them that do evil,<br>To cut off the remembrance of them from the earth.<br>

#### צ Zade
<sup>17</sup> _The righteous_ cry, and the LORD heareth,<br>And delivereth them out of all their troubles.<br>

#### ק Qoph
<sup>18</sup> The LORD _is_ nigh unto them that are of a broken heart;<sup>b</sup><br>And saveth such as be of a contrite spirit.<sup>c</sup><br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>19</sup> Many _are_ the afflictions of the righteous:<br>But the LORD delivereth him out of them all.<br>

#### ש Shin
<sup>20</sup> He keepeth all his bones:<br>Not one of them is broken.<br>

#### ת Taw
<sup>21</sup> Evil shall slay the wicked:<br>And they that hate the righteous shall be desolate.<sup>d</sup><br>
<sup>22</sup> The LORD redeemeth the soul of his servants:<br>And none of them that trust in him shall be desolate.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Or, _they flowed unto him_
> - <sup>b</sup> 18. Heb. _to the broken of heart_
> - <sup>c</sup> 18. Heb. _contrite of spirit_
> - <sup>d</sup> 21. Or, _shall be guilty_

## Chapter 35

> __A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> Plead _my cause_, O LORD, with them that strive with me:<br>Fight against them that fight against me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Take hold of shield and buckler,<br>And stand up for mine help.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Draw out also the spear, and stop _the way_ against them that persecute me:<br>Say unto my soul, I _am_ thy salvation.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Let them be confounded and put to shame that seek after my soul:<br>Let them be turned back and brought to confusion that devise my hurt.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let them be as chaff before the wind:<br>And let the angel of the LORD chase _them_.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Let their way be dark and slippery:<sup>a</sup><br>And let the angel of the LORD persecute them.<br>
<sup>7</sup> For without cause have they hid for me their net _in_ a pit,<br>_Which_ without cause they have digged for my soul.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Let destruction come upon him at unawares;<sup>b</sup> and let his net that he hath hid catch himself:<br>Into that very destruction let him fall.<br>
<sup>9</sup> And my soul shall be joyful in the LORD:<br>It shall rejoice in his salvation.<br>
<sup>10</sup> All my bones shall say, LORD, who _is_ like unto thee, which deliverest the poor from him that is too strong for him,<br>Yea, the poor and the needy from him that spoileth him?<br>
<sup>11</sup> False witnesses<sup>c</sup> did rise up;<br>They laid<sup>d</sup> to my charge _things_ that I knew not.<br>
<sup>12</sup> They rewarded me evil for good<br>_To_ the spoiling<sup>e</sup> of my soul.<br>
<sup>13</sup> But as for me, when they were sick, my clothing _was_ sackcloth: I humbled<sup>f</sup> my soul with fasting;<br>And my prayer returned into mine own bosom.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I behaved<sup>g</sup> myself as though _he had been_ my friend _or_ brother:<br>I bowed down heavily, as one that mourneth _for his_ mother.<br>
<sup>15</sup> But in mine adversity<sup>h</sup> they rejoiced, and gathered themselves together:<br>_Yea_, the abjects gathered themselves together against me, and I knew _it_ not; they did tear _me_, and ceased not:<br>
<sup>16</sup> With hypocritical mockers in feasts,<br>They gnashed upon me with their teeth.<br>
<sup>17</sup> Lord, how long wilt thou look on?<br>Rescue my soul from their destructions, my darling<sup>i</sup> from the lions.<br>
<sup>18</sup> I will give thee thanks in the great congregation:<br>I will praise thee among much<sup>j</sup> people.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Let not them that are mine enemies wrongfully<sup>k</sup> rejoice over me:<br>_Neither_ let them wink with the eye that hate me without a cause.<br>
<sup>20</sup> For they speak not peace:<br>But they devise deceitful matters against _them that are_ quiet in the land.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Yea, they opened their mouth wide against me,<br>_And_ said, Aha, aha, our eye hath seen _it_.<br>
<sup>22</sup> _This_ thou hast seen, O LORD: keep not silence:<br>O Lord, be not far from me.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Stir up thyself, and awake to my judgment,<br>_Even_ unto my cause, my God and my Lord.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Judge me, O LORD my God, according to thy righteousness;<br>And let them not rejoice over me.<br>
<sup>25</sup> Let them not say in their hearts, Ah, so would we have it:<sup>l</sup><br>Let them not say, We have swallowed him up.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Let them be ashamed and brought to confusion together that rejoice at mine hurt:<br>Let them be clothed with shame and dishonour that magnify _themselves_ against me.<br>
<sup>27</sup> Let them shout for joy, and be glad, that favour my righteous cause:<sup>m</sup><br>Yea, let them say continually, Let the LORD be magnified, which hath pleasure in the prosperity of his servant.<br>
<sup>28</sup> And my tongue shall speak of thy righteousness<br>_And_ of thy praise all the day long.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Heb. _darkness and slipperiness_
> - <sup>b</sup> 8. Heb. _which he knoweth not of_
> - <sup>c</sup> 11. Heb. _Witnesses of wrong_
> - <sup>d</sup> 11. Heb. _they asked me_
> - <sup>e</sup> 12. Heb. _depriving_
> - <sup>f</sup> 13. Or, _afflicted_
> - <sup>g</sup> 14. Heb. _walked_
> - <sup>h</sup> 15. Heb. _halting_
> - <sup>i</sup> 17. Heb. _my only one_
> - <sup>j</sup> 18. Heb. _strong_
> - <sup>k</sup> 19. Heb. _falsely_
> - <sup>l</sup> 25. Heb. _Ah, ah, our soul_
> - <sup>m</sup> 27. Heb. _my righteousness_

## Chapter 36

> _To the chief Musician, _A Psalm_ of David the servant of the LORD._

<sup>1</sup> The transgression of the wicked saith within my heart,<br>_That there is_ no fear of God before his eyes.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For he flattereth himself in his own eyes, until his iniquity be found to be hateful.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> The words of his mouth _are_ iniquity and deceit:<br>He hath left off to be wise, _and_ to do good.<br>
<sup>4</sup> He deviseth mischief<sup>b</sup> upon his bed;<br>He setteth himself in a way _that is_ not good;<br>He abhorreth not evil.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Thy mercy, O LORD, _is_ in the heavens;<br>_And_ thy faithfulness _reacheth_ unto the clouds.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Thy righteousness _is_ like the great mountains;<sup>c</sup><br>Thy judgments _are_ a great deep:<br>O LORD, thou preservest man and beast.<br>
<sup>7</sup> How excellent<sup>d</sup> _is_ thy lovingkindness, O God!<br>Therefore the children of men put their trust under the shadow of thy wings.<br>
<sup>8</sup> They shall be abundantly<sup>e</sup> satisfied with the fatness of thy house;<br>And thou shalt make them drink of the river of thy pleasures.<br>
<sup>9</sup> For with thee _is_ the fountain of life:<br>In thy light shall we see light.<br>
<sup>10</sup> O continue<sup>f</sup> thy lovingkindness unto them that know thee;<br>And thy righteousness to the upright in heart.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Let not the foot of pride come against me,<br>And let not the hand of the wicked remove me.<br>
<sup>12</sup> There are the workers of iniquity fallen:<br>They are cast down, and shall not be able to rise.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _to find his iniquity to hate_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Or, _vanity_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Heb. _the mountains of God_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Heb. _precious_
> - <sup>e</sup> 8. Heb. _watered_
> - <sup>f</sup> 10. Heb. _draw out at length_

## Chapter 37

> __A Psalm_ of David._

#### א Aleph

<sup>1</sup> Fret not thyself because of evildoers,<br>Neither be thou envious against the workers of iniquity.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For they shall soon be cut down like the grass,<br>And wither as the green herb.<br>

#### ב Bet
<sup>3</sup> Trust in the LORD, and do good;<br>_So_ shalt thou dwell in the land, and verily<sup>a</sup> thou shalt be fed.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Delight thyself also in the LORD;<br>And he shall give thee the desires of thine heart.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>5</sup> Commit thy way unto the LORD;<sup>b</sup> Trust also in him;<br>And he shall bring _it_ to pass.<br>
<sup>6</sup> And he shall bring forth thy righteousness as the light,<br>And thy judgment as the noonday.<br>

#### ד Dalet
<sup>7</sup> Rest in the LORD,<sup>c</sup> and wait patiently for him:<br>Fret not thyself because of him who prospereth in his way, because of the man who bringeth wicked devices to pass.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>8</sup> Cease from anger, and forsake wrath:<br>Fret not thyself in any wise to do evil.<br>
<sup>9</sup> For evildoers shall be cut off:<br>But those that wait upon the LORD, they shall inherit the earth.<br>

#### ו Waw
<sup>10</sup> For yet a little while, and the wicked _shall_ not _be:_<br>Yea, thou shalt diligently consider his place, and it _shall_ not _be_.<br>
<sup>11</sup> But the meek shall inherit the earth;<br>And shall delight themselves in the abundance of peace.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>12</sup> The wicked plotteth<sup>d</sup> against the just,<br>And gnasheth upon him with his teeth.<br>
<sup>13</sup> The Lord shall laugh at him:<br>For he seeth that his day is coming.<br>

#### ח Het
<sup>14</sup> The wicked have drawn out the sword, and have bent their bow,<br>To cast down the poor and needy, _and_ to slay such as be of upright conversation.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>15</sup> Their sword shall enter into their own heart,<br>And their bows shall be broken.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>16</sup> A little that a righteous man hath _is_ better<br>Than the riches of many wicked.<br>
<sup>17</sup> For the arms of the wicked shall be broken:<br>But the LORD upholdeth the righteous.<br>

#### י Yod
<sup>18</sup> The LORD knoweth the days of the upright:<br>And their inheritance shall be for ever.<br>
<sup>19</sup> They shall not be ashamed in the evil time:<br>And in the days of famine they shall be satisfied.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>20</sup> But the wicked shall perish,<br>And the enemies of the LORD _shall be_ as the fat of lambs:<sup>f</sup><br>They shall consume; into smoke shall they consume away.<br>

#### ל Lamed
<sup>21</sup> The wicked borroweth, and payeth not again:<br>But the righteous sheweth mercy, and giveth.<br>
<sup>22</sup> For _such as be_ blessed of him shall inherit the earth;<br>And _they that be_ cursed of him shall be cut off.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>23</sup> The steps of a _good_ man are ordered<sup>g</sup> by the LORD:<br>And he delighteth in his way.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Though he fall, he shall not be utterly cast down:<br>For the LORD upholdeth _him with_ his hand.<br>

#### נ Nun
<sup>25</sup> I have been young, and _now_ am old;<br>Yet have I not seen the righteous forsaken, nor his seed begging bread.<br>
<sup>26</sup> _He is_ ever<sup>h</sup> merciful, and lendeth;<br>And his seed _is_ blessed.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>27</sup> Depart from evil, and do good;<br>And dwell for evermore.<br>

#### ע Ain
<sup>28</sup> For the LORD loveth judgment,<br>And forsaketh not his saints; they are preserved for ever:<br>But the seed of the wicked shall be cut off.<br>
<sup>29</sup> The righteous shall inherit the land,<br>And dwell therein for ever.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>30</sup> The mouth of the righteous speaketh wisdom,<br>And his tongue talketh of judgment.<br>
<sup>31</sup> The law of his God _is_ in his heart;<br>None of his steps<sup>i</sup> shall slide.<br>

#### צ Zade
<sup>32</sup> The wicked watcheth the righteous,<br>And seeketh to slay him.<br>
<sup>33</sup> The LORD will not leave him in his hand,<br>Nor condemn him when he is judged.<br>

#### ק Qoph
<sup>34</sup> Wait on the LORD, and keep his way, and he shall exalt thee to inherit the land:<br>When the wicked are cut off, thou shalt see _it_.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>35</sup> I have seen the wicked in great power,<br>And spreading himself like a green bay tree.<sup>j</sup><br>
<sup>36</sup> Yet he passed away, and, lo, he _was_ not:<br>Yea, I sought him, but he could not be found.<br>

#### ש Shin
<sup>37</sup> Mark the perfect _man_, and behold the upright:<br>For the end of _that_ man _is_ peace.<br>
<sup>38</sup> But the transgressors shall be destroyed together:<br>The end of the wicked shall be cut off.<br>

#### ת Taw
<sup>39</sup> But the salvation of the righteous _is_ of the LORD:<br>_He is_ their strength in the time of trouble.<br>
<sup>40</sup> And the LORD shall help them, and deliver them:<br>He shall deliver them from the wicked, and save them, because they trust in him.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _in truth and stableness_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _roll thy way upon the Lord_
> - <sup>c</sup> 7. Heb. _be silent to the LORD_
> - <sup>d</sup> 12. Or, _practiseth_
> - <sup>e</sup> 14. Heb. _the upright of way_
> - <sup>f</sup> 20. Heb. _the preciousness of Lambs_
