<sup>38</sup> And shed innocent blood, _Even_ the blood of their sons and of their daughters,<br>Whom they sacrificed unto the idols of Canaan: And the land was polluted with blood.<br>
<sup>39</sup> Thus were they defiled with their own works,<br>And went a whoring with their own inventions.<br>
<sup>40</sup> Therefore was the wrath of the LORD kindled against his people,<br>Insomuch that he abhorred his own inheritance.<br>
<sup>41</sup> And he gave them into the hand of the heathen;<br>And they that hated them ruled over them.<br>
<sup>42</sup> Their enemies also oppressed them,<br>And they were brought into subjection under their hand.<br>
<sup>43</sup> Many times did he deliver them;<br>But they provoked _him_ with their counsel, and were brought low<sup>f</sup> for their iniquity.<br>
<sup>44</sup> Nevertheless he regarded their affliction,<br>When he heard their cry:<br>
<sup>45</sup> And he remembered for them his covenant, and repented according to the multitude of his mercies.<br>
<sup>46</sup> He made them also to be pitied of all those that carried them captives.<br>
<sup>47</sup> Save us, O LORD our God, and gather us from among the heathen,<br>To give thanks unto thy holy name, _and_ to triumph in thy praise.<br>
<sup>48</sup> Blessed _be_ the LORD God of Israel from everlasting to everlasting:<br>And let all the people say, Amen.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 13. Heb. _They made haste, they forgat_
> - <sup>c</sup> 14. Heb. _lusted a lust_
> - <sup>d</sup> 24. Heb. _a land of desire_
> - <sup>e</sup> 27. Heb. _to make them fall_
> - <sup>f</sup> 43. Or, _impoverished_, or _weakened_

## Chapter 107

<sup>1</sup> O give thanks unto the LORD, for _he is_ good:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let the redeemed of the LORD say _so_,<br>Whom he hath redeemed from the hand of the enemy;<br>
<sup>3</sup> And gathered them out of the lands, from the east, and from the west,<br>From the north, and from the south.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>4</sup> They wandered in the wilderness in a solitary way;<br>They found no city to dwell in.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Hungry and thirsty,<br>Their soul fainted in them.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Then they cried unto the LORD in their trouble,<br>_And_ he delivered them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>7</sup> And he led them forth by the right way,<br>That they might go to a city of habitation.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Oh that _men_ would praise the LORD _for_ his goodness,<br>And _for_ his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>9</sup> For he satisfieth the longing soul,<br>And filleth the hungry soul with goodness.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Such as sit in darkness and in the shadow of death,<br>_Being_ bound in affliction and iron;<br>
<sup>11</sup> Because they rebelled against the words of God,<br>And contemned the counsel of the Most High:<br>
<sup>12</sup> Therefore he brought down their heart with labour;<br>They fell down, and _there was_ none to help.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Then they cried unto the LORD in their trouble,<br>_And_ he saved them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>14</sup> He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death,<br>And brake their bands in sunder.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Oh that _men_ would praise the LORD _for_ his goodness,<br>And _for_ his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>16</sup> For he hath broken the gates of brass,<br>And cut the bars of iron in sunder.<br>
<sup>17</sup> Fools because of their transgression,<br>And because of their iniquities, are afflicted.<br>
<sup>18</sup> Their soul abhorreth all manner of meat;<br>And they draw near unto the gates of death.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Then they cry unto the LORD in their trouble,<br>_And_ he saveth them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>20</sup> He sent his word, and healed them,<br>And delivered _them_ from their destructions.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Oh that _men_ would praise the LORD _for_ his goodness,<br>And _for_ his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>22</sup> And let them sacrifice the sacrifices of thanksgiving,<br>And declare his works with rejoicing.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>23</sup> They that go down to the sea in ships,<br>That do business in great waters;<br>
<sup>24</sup> These see the works of the LORD,<br>And his wonders in the deep.<br>
<sup>25</sup> For he commandeth, and raiseth<sup>c</sup> the stormy wind,<br>Which lifteth up the waves thereof.<br>
<sup>26</sup> They mount up to the heaven, they go down again to the depths:<br>Their soul is melted because of trouble.<br>
<sup>27</sup> They reel to and fro, and stagger like a drunken man,<br>And are at their wits’ end.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Then they cry unto the LORD in their trouble,<br>And he bringeth them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>29</sup> He maketh the storm a calm,<br>So that the waves thereof are still.<br>
<sup>30</sup> Then are they glad because they be quiet;<br>So he bringeth them unto their desired haven.<br>
<sup>31</sup> Oh that _men_ would praise the LORD _for_ his goodness,<br>And _for_ his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>32</sup> Let them exalt him also in the congregation of the people,<br>And praise him in the assembly of the elders.<br>
<sup>33</sup> He turneth rivers into a wilderness,<br>And the watersprings into dry ground;<br>
<sup>34</sup> A fruitful land into barrenness,<sup>d</sup><br>For the wickedness of them that dwell therein.<br>
<sup>35</sup> He turneth the wilderness into a standing water,<br>And dry ground into watersprings.<br>
<sup>36</sup> And there he maketh the hungry<br>To dwell, that they may prepare a city for habitation;<br>
<sup>37</sup> And sow the fields, and plant vineyards,<br>Which may yield fruits of increase.<br>
<sup>38</sup> He blesseth them also, so that they are multiplied greatly;<br>And suffereth not their cattle to decrease.<br>
<sup>39</sup> Again, they are minished and brought low<br>Through oppression, affliction, and sorrow.<br>
<sup>40</sup> He poureth contempt upon princes,<br>And causeth them to wander in the wilderness,<sup>e</sup> _where there is_ no way.<br>
<sup>41</sup> Yet setteth he the poor on high from<sup>f</sup> affliction,<br>And maketh _him_ families like a flock.<br>
<sup>42</sup> The righteous shall see _it_, and rejoice:<br>And all iniquity shall stop her mouth.<br>
<sup>43</sup> Whoso _is_ wise, and will observe these _things_,<br>Even they shall understand the lovingkindness of the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _from the sea_
> - <sup>b</sup> 22. Heb. _singing_
> - <sup>c</sup> 25. Heb. _maketh to stand_
> - <sup>d</sup> 34. Heb. _saltiness_
> - <sup>e</sup> 40. Or, _void place_
> - <sup>f</sup> 41. Or, _after_

## Chapter 108

> _A Song _or_ Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> O God, my heart is fixed;<br>I will sing and give praise, even with my glory.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Awake, psaltery and harp:<br>I _myself_ will awake early.<br>
<sup>3</sup> I will praise thee, O LORD, among the people:<br>And I will sing praises unto thee among the nations.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For thy mercy _is_ great above the heavens:<br>And thy truth _reacheth_ unto the clouds.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>5</sup> Be thou exalted, O God, above the heavens:<br>And thy glory above all the earth;<br>
<sup>6</sup> That thy beloved may be delivered:<br>Save _with_ thy right hand, and answer me.<br>
<sup>7</sup> God hath spoken in his holiness;<br>I will rejoice, I will divide Shechem, and mete out the valley of Succoth.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Gilead _is_ mine; Manasseh _is_ mine;<br>Ephraim also _is_ the strength of mine head;<br>Judah _is_ my lawgiver;
<sup>9</sup> Moab _is_ my washpot;<br>Over Edom will I cast out my shoe; Over Philistia will I triumph.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Who will bring me into the strong city?<br>Who will lead me into Edom?<br>
<sup>11</sup> _Wilt_ not _thou_, O God, _who_ hast cast us off?<br>And wilt not thou, O God, go forth with our hosts?<br>
<sup>12</sup> Give us help from trouble:<br>For vain _is_ the help of man.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Through God we shall do valiantly:<br>For he _it is that_ shall tread down our enemies.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Or, _skies_

## Chapter 109

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Hold not thy peace, O God of my praise;<br>
<sup>2</sup> For the mouth of the wicked and the mouth of the deceitful<sup>a</sup> are opened<sup>b</sup> against me:<br>They have spoken against me with a lying tongue.<br>
<sup>3</sup> They compassed me about also with words of hatred; And fought against me without a cause.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For my love they are my adversaries:<br>But I _give myself unto_ prayer.<br>
<sup>5</sup> And they have rewarded me evil for good,<br>And hatred for my love.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Set thou a wicked man over him:<br>And let Satan<sup>c</sup> stand at his right hand.<br>
<sup>7</sup> When he shall be judged, let him be condemned:<sup>d</sup><br>And let his prayer become sin.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Let his days be few;<br>_And_ let another take his office.<sup>e</sup><br>
<sup>9</sup> Let his children be fatherless,<br>And his wife a widow.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let his children be continually vagabonds, and beg:<br>Let them seek _their bread_ also out of their desolate places.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Let the extortioner catch all that he hath;<br>And let the strangers spoil his labour.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Let there be none to extend mercy unto him:<br>Neither let there be any to favour his fatherless children.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Let his posterity be cut off;<br>_And_ in the generation following let their name be blotted out.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Let the iniquity of his fathers be remembered with the LORD;<br>And let not the sin of his mother be blotted out.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Let them be before the LORD continually,<br>That he may cut off the memory of them from the earth.<br>
<sup>16</sup> Because that he remembered not to shew mercy,<br>But persecuted the poor and needy man, That he might even slay the broken in heart.<br>
<sup>17</sup> As he loved cursing, so let it come unto him:<br>As he delighted not in blessing, so let it be far from him.<br>
<sup>18</sup> As he clothed himself with cursing like as with his garment,<br>So let it come into his bowels<sup>f</sup> like water, and like oil into his bones.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Let it be unto him as the garment _which_ covereth him,<br>And for a girdle wherewith he is girded continually.<br>
<sup>20</sup> _Let_ this _be_ the reward of mine adversaries from the LORD,<br>And of them that speak evil against my soul.<br>
<sup>21</sup> But do thou for me, O GOD the Lord, for thy name’s sake:<br>Because thy mercy _is_ good, deliver thou me.<br>
<sup>22</sup> For I _am_ poor and needy,<br>And my heart is wounded within me.<br>
<sup>23</sup> I am gone like the shadow when it declineth:<br>I am tossed up and down as the locust.<br>
<sup>24</sup> My knees are weak through fasting;<br>And my flesh faileth of fatness.<br>
<sup>25</sup> I became also a reproach unto them:<br>_When_ they looked upon me they shaked their heads.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Help me, O LORD my God:<br>O save me according to thy mercy:<br>
<sup>27</sup> That they may know that this _is_ thy hand;<br>_That_ thou, LORD, hast done it.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Let them curse, but bless thou:<br>When they arise, let them be ashamed; but let thy servant rejoice.<br>
<sup>29</sup> Let mine adversaries be clothed with shame,<br>And let them cover themselves with their own confusion, as with a mantle.<br>
<sup>30</sup> I will greatly praise the LORD with my mouth;<br>Yea, I will praise him among the multitude.<br>
<sup>31</sup> For he shall stand at the right hand of the poor,<br>To save _him_ from those that condemn his soul.<sup>g</sup><br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Heb. _of deceit_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Heb. _have opened themselves_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Or, _an adversary_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Heb. _go out guilty_, or _wicked_
> - <sup>e</sup> 8. Or, _charge_
> - <sup>f</sup> 18. Heb. _within him_
> - <sup>g</sup> 31. Heb. _from the judges of his soul_

## Chapter 110

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> The LORD said unto my Lord,<br>Sit thou at my right hand, until I make thine enemies thy footstool.<br>
<sup>2</sup> The LORD shall send the rod of thy strength out of Zion:<br>Rule thou in the midst of thine enemies.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thy people _shall be_ willing in the day of thy power,<br>In the beauties of holiness from the womb of the morning: Thou hast the dew of thy youth.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The LORD hath sworn, and will not repent,<br>Thou _art_ a priest for ever after the order of Melchizedek.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The Lord at thy right hand<br>Shall strike through kings in the day of his wrath.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He shall judge among the heathen, he shall fill _the places_ with the dead bodies;<br>He shall wound the heads over many<sup>a</sup> countries.<br>
<sup>7</sup> He shall drink of the brook in the way:<br>Therefore shall he lift up the head.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Or, _great_

## Chapter 111

#### א Aleph


#### ב Bet
<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD. I will praise the LORD with _my_ whole heart,<br>In the assembly of the upright, and _in_ the congregation.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>2</sup> 
#### ד Dalet
The works of the LORD _are_ great,<br>Sought out of all them that have pleasure therein.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>3</sup> 
#### ו Waw
His work _is_ honourable and glorious:<br>And his righteousness endureth for ever.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>4</sup> 
#### ח Het
He hath made his wonderful works to be remembered:<br>The LORD _is_ gracious and full of compassion.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>5</sup> 
#### י Yod
He hath given meat<sup>b</sup> unto them that fear him:<br>He will ever be mindful of his covenant.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>6</sup> 
#### ל Lamed
He hath shewed his people the power of his works,<br>That he may give them the heritage of the heathen.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>7</sup> 
#### נ Nun
The works of his hands _are_ verity and judgment;<br>All his commandments _are_ sure.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>8</sup> 
#### ע Ain
They stand fast<sup>c</sup> for ever and ever,<br>_And are_ done in truth and uprightness.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>9</sup> 
#### צ Zade

#### ק Qoph
He sent redemption unto his people:<br>He hath commanded his covenant for ever:<br>Holy and reverend _is_ his name.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>10</sup> 
#### ש Shin

#### ת Taw
The fear of the LORD _is_ the beginning of wisdom:<br>A good understanding<sup>d</sup> have all they that do _his commandments:_<sup>e</sup><br>His praise endureth for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _prey_
> - <sup>c</sup> 8. Heb. _are established_
> - <sup>d</sup> 10. Or, _good success_
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Heb. _that do them_

## Chapter 112

#### א Aleph


#### ב Bet
<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD. Blessed _is_ the man _that_ feareth the LORD,<br>_That_ delighteth greatly in his commandments.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>2</sup> 
#### ד Dalet
His seed shall be mighty upon earth:<br>The generation of the upright shall be blessed.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>3</sup> 
#### ו Waw
Wealth and riches _shall be_ in his house:<br>And his righteousness endureth for ever.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>4</sup> 
#### ח Het
Unto the upright there ariseth light in the darkness:<br>_He is_ gracious, and full of compassion, and righteous.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>5</sup> 
#### י Yod
A good man sheweth favour, and lendeth:<br>He will guide his affairs with discretion.<sup>b</sup><br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>6</sup> 
#### ל Lamed
Surely he shall not be moved for ever:<br>The righteous shall be in everlasting remembrance.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>7</sup> 
#### נ Nun
He shall not be afraid of evil tidings:<br>His heart is fixed, trusting in the LORD.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>8</sup> 
#### ע Ain
His heart _is_ established, he shall not be afraid,<br>Until he see _his desire_ upon his enemies.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>9</sup> 
#### צ Zade

#### ק Qoph
He hath dispersed, he hath given to the poor;<br>His righteousness endureth for ever;<br>His horn shall be exalted with honour.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>10</sup> 
#### ש Shin

#### ת Taw
The wicked shall see _it_, and be grieved;<br>He shall gnash with his teeth, and melt away:<br>The desire of the wicked shall perish.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _judgment_

## Chapter 113

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD. Praise, O ye servants of the LORD,<br>Praise the name of the LORD.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Blessed be the name of the LORD<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<br>
<sup>3</sup> From the rising of the sun unto the going down of the same<br>The LORD’s name _is_ to be praised.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The LORD _is_ high above all nations,<br>_And_ his glory above the heavens.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Who _is_ like unto the LORD our God, who dwelleth on high,<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>6</sup> Who humbleth _himself_ to behold _the things that are_ in heaven, and in the earth!<br>
<sup>7</sup> He raiseth up the poor out of the dust,<br>_And_ lifteth the needy out of the dunghill;<br>
<sup>8</sup> That he may set _him_ with princes,<br>_Even_ with the princes of his people.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He maketh the barren woman to keep house,<sup>c</sup><br>_And to be_ a joyful mother of children.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _exalteth himself to dwell_
> - <sup>c</sup> 9. Heb. _to dwell in an house_

## Chapter 114

<sup>1</sup> When Israel went out of Egypt,<br>The house of Jacob from a people of strange language;<br>
<sup>2</sup> Judah was his sanctuary,<br>_And_ Israel his dominion.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The sea saw _it_, and fled:<br>Jordan was driven back.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The mountains skipped like rams,<br>_And_ the little hills like lambs.<br>
<sup>5</sup> What _ailed_ thee, O thou sea, that thou fleddest?<br>Thou Jordan, _that_ thou wast driven back?<br>
<sup>6</sup> Ye mountains, _that_ ye skipped like rams;<br>_And_ ye little hills, like lambs?<br>
<sup>7</sup> Tremble, thou earth, at the presence of the Lord,<br>At the presence of the God of Jacob;<br>
<sup>8</sup> Which turned the rock _into_ a standing water,<br>The flint into a fountain of waters.<br>

## Chapter 115

<sup>1</sup> Not unto us, O LORD, not unto us, but unto thy name give glory,<br>For thy mercy, _and_ for thy truth’s sake.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Wherefore should the heathen say,<br>Where _is_ now their God?<br>
<sup>3</sup> But our God _is_ in the heavens:<br>He hath done whatsoever he hath pleased.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Their idols _are_ silver and gold,<br>The work of men’s hands.<br>
<sup>5</sup> They have mouths, but they speak not:<br>Eyes have they, but they see not:<br>
<sup>6</sup> They have ears, but they hear not:<br>Noses have they, but they smell not:<br>
<sup>7</sup> They have hands, but they handle not: Feet have they, but they walk not:<br>Neither speak they through their throat.<br>
<sup>8</sup> They that make them are like unto them;<br>_So is_ every one that trusteth in them.<br>
<sup>9</sup> O Israel, trust thou in the LORD:<br>He _is_ their help and their shield.<br>
<sup>10</sup> O house of Aaron, trust in the LORD:<br>He _is_ their help and their shield.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Ye that fear the LORD, trust in the LORD:<br>He _is_ their help and their shield.<br>
<sup>12</sup> The LORD hath been mindful of us: he will bless _us_;<br>He will bless the house of Israel; He will bless the house of Aaron.<br>
<sup>13</sup> He will bless them that fear<br>the LORD, _both_<sup>a</sup> small and great.<br>
<sup>14</sup> The LORD shall increase you more and more,<br>You and your children.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Ye _are_ blessed of the LORD<br>Which made heaven and earth.<br>
<sup>16</sup> The heaven, _even_ the heavens, _are_ the LORD’s:<br>But the earth hath he given to the children of men.<br>
<sup>17</sup> The dead praise not the LORD,<br>Neither any that go down into silence.<br>
<sup>18</sup> But we will bless the LORD<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<br>Praise the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 13. Heb. _with_

## Chapter 116

<sup>1</sup> I love the LORD,<br>Because he hath heard my voice _and_ my supplications.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Because he hath inclined his ear unto me,<br>Therefore will I call upon _him_ as long as I live.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The sorrows of death compassed me,<br>And the pains of hell gat hold<sup>a</sup> upon me:<br>I found trouble and sorrow.
<sup>4</sup> Then called I upon the name of the LORD;<br>O LORD, I beseech thee, deliver my soul.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Gracious _is_ the LORD, and righteous;<br>Yea, our God _is_ merciful.<br>
<sup>6</sup> The LORD preserveth the simple:<br>I was brought low, and he helped me.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Return unto thy rest, O my soul;<br>For the LORD hath dealt bountifully with thee.<br>
<sup>8</sup> For thou hast delivered my soul from death,<br>Mine eyes from tears, _and_ my feet from falling.<br>
<sup>9</sup> I will walk before<br>The LORD in the land of the living.<br>
<sup>10</sup> I believed, therefore have I spoken: I was greatly afflicted:<br>
<sup>11</sup> I said in my haste, all men _are_ liars.<br>
<sup>12</sup> What shall I render unto the LORD<br>_For_ all his benefits toward me?<br>
<sup>13</sup> I will take the cup of salvation,<br>And call upon the name of the LORD.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I will pay my vows unto the LORD now in the presence of all his people.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Precious in the sight of the LORD _is_ the death of his saints.<br>
<sup>16</sup> O LORD, truly I _am_ thy servant;<br>I _am_ thy servant, _and_ the son of thine handmaid: Thou hast loosed my bonds.<br>
<sup>17</sup> I will offer to thee the sacrifice of thanksgiving,<br>And will call upon the name of the LORD.<br>
<sup>18</sup> I will pay my vows unto the LORD now in the presence of all his people,<br>
<sup>19</sup> In the courts of the LORD’s house, in the midst of thee, O Jerusalem.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _found me_

## Chapter 117

<sup>1</sup> O praise the LORD,<br>All ye nations: Praise him, all ye people.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For his merciful kindness is great toward us:<br>And the truth of the LORD _endureth_ for ever.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

## Chapter 118

<sup>1</sup> O give thanks unto the LORD; for _he is_ good:<br>Because his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let Israel now say,<br>That his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Let the house of Aaron now say,<br>That his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Let them now that fear the LORD say,<br>That his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I called upon the LORD in distress:<sup>a</sup><br>The LORD answered me, _and set me_ in a large place.<br>
<sup>6</sup> The LORD _is_ on my side;<sup>b</sup><br>I will not fear: What can man do unto me?<br>
<sup>7</sup> The LORD taketh my part with them that help me:<br>Therefore shall I see _my desire_ upon them that hate me.<br>
<sup>8</sup> _It is_ better to trust in the LORD<br>Than to put confidence in man.<br>
<sup>9</sup> _It is_ better to trust in the LORD<br>Than to put confidence in princes.<br>
<sup>10</sup> All nations compassed me about:<br>But in the name of the LORD will I destroy them.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>11</sup> They compassed me about; yea, they compassed me about:<br>But in the name of the LORD I will destroy them.<br>
<sup>12</sup> They compassed me about like bees; they are quenched as the fire of thorns:<br>For in the name of the LORD I will destroy<sup>d</sup> them.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Thou hast thrust sore at me that I might fall:<br>But the LORD helped me.<br>
<sup>14</sup> The LORD _is_ my strength and song,<br>And is become my salvation.<br>
<sup>15</sup> The voice of rejoicing and salvation _is_ in the tabernacles of the righteous:<br>The right hand of the LORD doeth valiantly.<br>
<sup>16</sup> The right hand of the LORD is exalted:<br>The right hand of the LORD doeth valiantly.<br>
<sup>17</sup> I shall not die, but live,<br>And declare the works of the LORD.<br>
<sup>18</sup> The LORD hath chastened me sore:<br>But he hath not given me over unto death.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Open to me the gates of righteousness:<br>I will go into them, _and_ I will praise the LORD:<br>
<sup>20</sup> This gate of the LORD,<br>Into which the righteous shall enter.<br>
<sup>21</sup> I will praise thee: for thou hast heard me,<br>And art become my salvation.<br>
<sup>22</sup> The stone _which_ the builders refused<br>Is become the head _stone_ of the corner.<br>
<sup>23</sup> This is the LORD’s doing;<br>It _is_ marvellous in our eyes.<br>
<sup>24</sup> This _is_ the day _which_ the LORD hath made;<br>We will rejoice and be glad in it.<br>
<sup>25</sup> Save now, I beseech thee, O LORD:<br>O LORD, I beseech thee, send now prosperity.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Blessed _be_ he that cometh in the name of the LORD:<br>We have blessed you out of the house of the LORD.<br>
<sup>27</sup> God _is_ the LORD, which hath shewed us light:<br>Bind the sacrifice with cords, _even_ unto the horns of the altar.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Thou _art_ my God, and I will praise thee:<br>_Thou art_ my God, I will exalt thee.<br>
<sup>29</sup> O give thanks unto the LORD; for _he is_ good:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Heb. _out of distress_
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Heb. _for me_
> - <sup>c</sup> 10. Heb. _cut them off_
> - <sup>d</sup> 12. Heb. _cut down_

## Chapter 119

#### א Aleph

<sup>1</sup> Blessed _are_ the undefiled<sup>a</sup> in the way,<br>Who walk in the law of the LORD.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Blessed _are_ they that keep his testimonies,<br>_And that_ seek him with the whole heart.<br>
<sup>3</sup> They also do no iniquity:<br>They walk in his ways.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Thou hast commanded _us_<br>To keep thy precepts diligently.<br>
<sup>5</sup> O that my ways were directed<br>To keep thy statutes!<br>
<sup>6</sup> Then shall I not be ashamed,<br>When I have respect unto all thy commandments.<br>
<sup>7</sup> I will praise thee with uprightness of heart,<br>When I shall have learned thy righteous judgments.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>8</sup> I will keep thy statutes:<br>O forsake me not utterly.<br>

#### ב Bet
<sup>9</sup> Wherewithal shall a young man cleanse his way?<br>By taking heed _thereto_ according to thy word.<br>
<sup>10</sup> With my whole heart have I sought thee:<br>O let me not wander from thy commandments.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Thy word have I hid in mine heart,<br>That I might not sin against thee.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Blessed _art_ thou, O LORD:<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>13</sup> With my lips have I declared<br>All the judgments of thy mouth.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I have rejoiced in the way of thy testimonies,<br>As _much as_ in all riches.<br>
<sup>15</sup> I will meditate in thy precepts,<br>And have respect unto thy ways.<br>
<sup>16</sup> I will delight myself in thy statutes:<br>I will not forget thy word.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>17</sup> Deal bountifully with thy servant,<br>_That_ I may live, and keep thy word.<br>
<sup>18</sup> Open<sup>c</sup> thou mine eyes,<br>That I may behold wondrous things out of thy law.<br>
<sup>19</sup> I _am_ a stranger in the earth:<br>Hide not thy commandments from me.<br>
<sup>20</sup> My soul breaketh for the longing<br>_That it hath_ unto thy judgments at all times.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Thou hast rebuked the proud<br>_That are_ cursed, which do err from thy commandments.<br>
<sup>22</sup> Remove from me reproach and contempt:<br>For I have kept thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Princes also did sit _and_ speak against me:<br>_But_ thy servant did meditate in thy statutes.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Thy testimonies also _are_<br>My delight _and_ my counsellors.<sup>d</sup><br>

#### ד Dalet
<sup>25</sup> My soul cleaveth unto the dust:<br>Quicken thou me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>26</sup> I have declared my ways, and thou heardest me:<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>27</sup> Make me to understand the way of thy precepts:<br>So shall I talk of thy wondrous works.<br>
<sup>28</sup> My soul melteth<sup>e</sup> for heaviness:<br>Strengthen thou me according unto thy word.<br>
<sup>29</sup> Remove from me the way of lying:<br>And grant me thy law graciously.<br>
<sup>30</sup> I have chosen the way of truth:<br>Thy judgments have I laid _before me_.<br>
<sup>31</sup> I have stuck unto thy testimonies:<br>O LORD, put me not to shame.<br>
<sup>32</sup> I will run the way of thy commandments,<br>When thou shalt enlarge my heart.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>33</sup> Teach me, O LORD, the way of thy statutes;<br>And I shall keep it _unto_ the end.<br>
<sup>34</sup> Give me understanding, and I shall keep thy law;<br>Yea, I shall observe it with _my_ whole heart.<br>
<sup>35</sup> Make me to go in the path of thy commandments;<br>For therein do I delight.<br>
<sup>36</sup> Incline my heart unto thy testimonies,<br>And not to covetousness.<br>
<sup>37</sup> Turn away<sup>f</sup> mine eyes from beholding vanity;<br>_And_ quicken thou me in thy way.<br>
<sup>38</sup> Stablish thy word<br>Unto thy servant, who _is devoted_ to thy fear.<br>
<sup>39</sup> Turn away my reproach which I fear:<br>For thy judgments _are_ good.<br>
<sup>40</sup> Behold, I have longed after thy precepts:<br>Quicken me in thy righteousness.<br>

#### ו Waw
<sup>41</sup> Let thy mercies come also unto me, O LORD,<br>_Even_ thy salvation, according to thy word.<br>
<sup>42</sup> So shall I have wherewith to answer him that reproacheth me:<br>For I trust in thy word.<br>
<sup>43</sup> And take not the word of truth utterly out of my mouth;<br>For I have hoped in thy judgments.<br>
<sup>44</sup> So shall I keep<br>thy law continually for ever and ever.<br>
<sup>45</sup> And I will walk at liberty:<sup>g</sup><br>For I seek thy precepts.<br>
<sup>46</sup> I will speak of thy testimonies also before kings,<br>And will not be ashamed.<br>
<sup>47</sup> And I will delight myself<br>In thy commandments, which I have loved.<br>
<sup>48</sup> My hands also will I lift up unto thy commandments, which I have loved;<br>And I will meditate in thy statutes.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>49</sup> Remember the word unto thy servant,<br>Upon which thou hast caused me to hope.<br>
<sup>50</sup> This _is_ my comfort in my affliction:<br>For thy word hath quickened me.<br>
<sup>51</sup> The proud have had me greatly in derision:<br>_Yet_ have I not declined from thy law.<br>
<sup>52</sup> I remembered thy judgments of old,<br>O LORD; And have comforted myself.<br>
<sup>53</sup> Horror hath taken hold upon me<br>Because of the wicked that forsake thy law.<br>
<sup>54</sup> Thy statutes have been my songs<br>In the house of my pilgrimage.<br>
<sup>55</sup> I have remembered thy name, O LORD, in the night, and have kept thy law.<br>
<sup>56</sup> This I had, because I kept thy precepts.<br>

#### ח Het
<sup>57</sup> _Thou art_ my portion, O LORD:<br>I have said that I would keep thy words.<br>
<sup>58</sup> I intreated thy favour<sup>h</sup> with _my_ whole heart:<br>Be merciful unto me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>59</sup> I thought on my ways,<br>And turned my feet unto thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>60</sup> I made haste, and delayed not<br>To keep thy commandments.<br>
<sup>61</sup> The bands<sup>i</sup> of the wicked have robbed me:<br>_But_ I have not forgotten thy law.<br>
<sup>62</sup> At midnight I will rise to give thanks unto thee<br>Because of thy righteous judgments.<br>
<sup>63</sup> I _am_ a companion of all _them_<br>That fear thee, and of them that keep thy precepts.<br>
<sup>64</sup> The earth, O LORD, is full of<br>Thy mercy: Teach me thy statutes.<br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>65</sup> Thou hast dealt well with thy servant,<br>O LORD, according unto thy word.<br>
<sup>66</sup> Teach me good judgment and knowledge:<br>For I have believed thy commandments.<br>
<sup>67</sup> Before I was afflicted I went astray:<br>But now have I kept thy word.<br>
<sup>68</sup> Thou _art_ good, and doest good;<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>69</sup> The proud have forged a lie against me:<br>_But_ I will keep thy precepts with _my_ whole heart.<br>
<sup>70</sup> Their heart is as fat as grease;<br>_But_ I delight in thy law.<br>
<sup>71</sup> _It is_ good for me that I have been afflicted;<br>That I might learn thy statutes.<br>
<sup>72</sup> The law of thy mouth _is_ better unto me<br>Than thousands of gold and silver.<br>

#### י Yod
<sup>73</sup> Thy hands have made me and fashioned me:<br>Give me understanding, that I may learn thy commandments.<br>
<sup>74</sup> They that fear thee will be glad when they see me;<br>Because I have hoped in thy word.<br>
<sup>75</sup> I know, O LORD, that thy judgments _are_ right,<sup>j</sup><br>And _that_ thou in faithfulness hast afflicted me.<br>
<sup>76</sup> Let, I pray thee, thy merciful kindness be for my comfort,<sup>k</sup><br>According to thy word unto thy servant.<br>
<sup>77</sup> Let thy tender mercies come unto me, that I may live:<br>For thy law _is_ my delight.<br>
<sup>78</sup> Let the proud be ashamed; for they dealt perversely with me without a cause:<br>_But_ I will meditate in thy precepts.<br>
<sup>79</sup> Let those that fear thee turn unto me,<br>And those that have known thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>80</sup> Let my heart be sound in thy statutes;<br>That I be not ashamed.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>81</sup> My soul fainteth for thy salvation:<br>_But_ I hope in thy word.<br>
<sup>82</sup> Mine eyes fail for thy word,<br>Saying, When wilt thou comfort me?<br>
<sup>83</sup> For I am become like a bottle in the smoke;<br>_Yet_ do I not forget thy statutes.<br>
<sup>84</sup> How many _are_ the days of thy servant?<br>When wilt thou execute judgment on them that persecute me?<br>
<sup>85</sup> The proud have digged pits for me,<br>Which _are_ not after thy law.<br>
<sup>86</sup> All thy commandments _are_ faithful:<sup>l</sup><br>They persecute me wrongfully; help thou me.<br>
<sup>87</sup> They had almost consumed me upon earth;<br>But I forsook not thy precepts.<br>
<sup>88</sup> Quicken me after thy lovingkindness;<br>So shall I keep the testimony of thy mouth.<br>

#### ל Lamed
<sup>89</sup> For ever, O LORD, Thy word<br>Is settled in heaven.<br>
<sup>90</sup> Thy faithfulness _is_ unto all generations:<sup>m</sup><br>Thou hast established the earth, and it abideth.<sup>n</sup><br>
<sup>91</sup> They continue this day according to thine ordinances:<br>For all _are_ thy servants.<br>
<sup>92</sup> Unless thy law _had been_ my delights,<br>I should then have perished in mine affliction.<br>
<sup>93</sup> I will never forget thy precepts:<br>For with them thou hast quickened me.<br>
<sup>94</sup> I _am_ thine, save me;<br>For I have sought thy precepts.<br>
<sup>95</sup> The wicked have waited for me to destroy me:<br>_But_ I will consider thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>96</sup> I have seen an end of all perfection:<br>_But_ thy commandment _is_ exceeding broad.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>97</sup> O how love I thy law!<br>It _is_ my meditation all the day.<br>
<sup>98</sup> Thou through thy commandments hast made me wiser than mine enemies:<br>For they _are_ ever with me.<sup>o</sup><br>
<sup>99</sup> I have more understanding than all my teachers:<br>For thy testimonies _are_ my meditation.<br>
<sup>100</sup> I understand more than the ancients,<br>Because I keep thy precepts.<br>
<sup>101</sup> I have refrained my feet from every evil way,<br>That I might keep thy word.<br>
<sup>102</sup> I have not departed<br>From thy judgments: For thou hast taught me.<br>
<sup>103</sup> How sweet are thy words unto my taste!<sup>p</sup><br>_Yea, sweeter_ than honey to my mouth!<br>
<sup>104</sup> Through thy precepts I get understanding:<br>Therefore I hate every false way.<br>

#### נ Nun
<sup>105</sup> Thy word _is_ a lamp<sup>q</sup> unto my feet,<br>And a light unto my path.<br>
<sup>106</sup> I have sworn, and I will perform _it_,<br>That I will keep thy righteous judgments.<br>
<sup>107</sup> I am afflicted very much:<br>Quicken me, O LORD, according unto thy word.<br>
<sup>108</sup> Accept, I beseech thee, the freewill offerings of my mouth, O LORD,<br>And teach me thy judgments.<br>
<sup>109</sup> My soul _is_ continually in my hand:<br>Yet do I not forget thy law.<br>
<sup>110</sup> The wicked have laid a snare for me:<br>Yet I erred not from thy precepts.<br>
<sup>111</sup> Thy testimonies have I taken as an heritage for ever:<br>For they _are_ the rejoicing of my heart.<br>
<sup>112</sup> I have inclined mine heart to perform<sup>r</sup> thy statutes<br>Alway, _even unto_ the end.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>113</sup> I hate _vain_ thoughts:<br>But thy law do I love.<br>
<sup>114</sup> Thou _art_ my hiding place and my shield:<br>I hope in thy word.<br>
<sup>115</sup> Depart from me, ye evildoers:<br>For I will keep the commandments of my God.<br>
<sup>116</sup> Uphold me according unto thy word, that I may live:<br>And let me not be ashamed of my hope.<br>
<sup>117</sup> Hold thou me up, and I shall be safe:<br>And I will have respect unto thy statutes continually.<br>
<sup>118</sup> Thou hast trodden down all them that err from thy statutes:<br>For their deceit _is_ falsehood.<br>
<sup>119</sup> Thou puttest away<sup>s</sup> all the wicked of the earth _like_ dross:<br>Therefore I love thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>120</sup> My flesh trembleth for fear of thee;<br>And I am afraid of thy judgments.<br>

#### ע Ain
<sup>121</sup> I have done judgment and justice:<br>Leave me not to mine oppressors.<br>
<sup>122</sup> Be surety for thy servant for good:<br>Let not the proud oppress me.<br>
<sup>123</sup> Mine eyes fail for thy salvation,<br>And for the word of thy righteousness.<br>
<sup>124</sup> Deal with thy servant according unto thy mercy,<br>And teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>125</sup> I _am_ thy servant; give me understanding,<br>That I may know thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>126</sup> _It is_ time for _thee,_ _LORD_, to work:<br>_For_ they have made void thy law.<br>
<sup>127</sup> Therefore I love thy commandments<br>Above gold; yea, above fine gold.<br>
<sup>128</sup> Therefore I esteem all _thy_ precepts _concerning_ all _things to be_ right;<br>_And_ I hate every false way.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>129</sup> Thy testimonies _are_ wonderful:<br>Therefore doth my soul keep them.<br>
<sup>130</sup> The entrance of thy words giveth light;<br>It giveth understanding unto the simple.<br>
<sup>131</sup> I opened my mouth, and panted:<br>For I longed for thy commandments.<br>
<sup>132</sup> Look thou upon me, and be merciful unto me,<br>As thou usest to do unto those<sup>t</sup> that love thy name.<br>
<sup>133</sup> Order my steps in thy word:<br>And let not any iniquity have dominion over me.<br>
<sup>134</sup> Deliver me from the oppression of man:<br>So will I keep thy precepts.<br>
<sup>135</sup> Make thy face to shine upon thy servant;<br>And teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>136</sup> Rivers of waters run down mine eyes,<br>Because they keep not thy law.<br>

#### צ Zade
<sup>137</sup> Righteous _art_ thou, O LORD,<br>And upright _are_ thy judgments.<br>
<sup>138</sup> Thy testimonies _that_ thou hast commanded<br>_are_ righteous<sup>u</sup> and very faithful.<sup>v</sup><br>
<sup>139</sup> My zeal hath consumed me,<sup>w</sup><br>Because mine enemies have forgotten thy words.<br>
<sup>140</sup> Thy word _is_ very pure:<sup>x</sup><br>Therefore thy servant loveth it.<br>
<sup>141</sup> I _am_ small and despised:<br>_Yet_ do not I forget thy precepts.<br>
<sup>142</sup> Thy righteousness _is_ an everlasting righteousness,<br>And thy law _is_ the truth.<br>
<sup>143</sup> Trouble and anguish have taken hold<sup>y</sup> on me:<br>_Yet_ thy commandments _are_ my delights.<br>
<sup>144</sup> The righteousness of thy testimonies _is_ everlasting:<br>Give me understanding, and I shall live.<br>

#### ק Qoph
<sup>145</sup> I cried with _my_ whole heart;<br>hear me, O LORD: I will keep thy statutes.<br>
<sup>146</sup> I cried unto thee;<br>save me, and I shall keep thy testimonies.<sup>z</sup><br>
<sup>147</sup> I prevented the dawning of the morning, and cried:<br>I hoped in thy word.<br>
<sup>148</sup> Mine eyes prevent the _night_ watches,<br>That I might meditate in thy word.<br>
<sup>149</sup> Hear my voice according unto thy lovingkindness:<br>O LORD, quicken me according to thy judgment.<br>
<sup>150</sup> They draw nigh that follow after mischief:<br>They are far from thy law.<br>
<sup>151</sup> Thou _art_ near, O LORD;<br>And all thy commandments _are_ truth.<br>
<sup>152</sup> Concerning thy testimonies, I have known of old<br>That thou hast founded them for ever.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>153</sup> Consider mine affliction, and deliver me:<br>For I do not forget thy law.<br>
<sup>154</sup> Plead my cause, and deliver me:<br>Quicken me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>155</sup> Salvation _is_ far from the wicked:<br>For they seek not thy statutes.<br>
<sup>156</sup> Great<sup>aa</sup> _are_ thy tender mercies, O LORD:<br>Quicken me according to thy judgments.<br>
<sup>157</sup> Many _are_ my persecutors and mine enemies;<br>_Yet_ do I not decline from thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>158</sup> I beheld the transgressors, and was grieved;<br>Because they kept not thy word.<br>
<sup>159</sup> Consider how I love thy precepts:<br>Quicken me, O LORD, according to thy lovingkindness.<br>
<sup>160</sup> Thy word _is_ true _from_ the beginning:<sup>ab</sup><br>And every one of thy righteous judgments _endureth_ for ever.<br>

#### ש Shin
<sup>161</sup> Princes have persecuted me without a cause:<br>But my heart standeth in awe of thy word.<br>
<sup>162</sup> I rejoice at thy word,<br>As one that findeth great spoil.<br>
<sup>163</sup> I hate and abhor lying:<br>_But_ thy law do I love.<br>
<sup>164</sup> Seven times a day do I praise thee<br>Because of thy righteous judgments.<br>
<sup>165</sup> Great peace have they which love thy law:<br>And nothing shall offend them.<sup>ac</sup><br>
<sup>166</sup> LORD, I have hoped for thy salvation,<br>And done thy commandments.<br>
<sup>167</sup> My soul hath kept thy testimonies;<br>And I love them exceedingly.<br>
<sup>168</sup> I have kept thy precepts and thy testimonies:<br>For all my ways _are_ before thee.<br>

#### ת Taw
<sup>169</sup> Let my cry come near before thee, O LORD:<br>Give me understanding according to thy word.<br>
<sup>170</sup> Let my supplication come before thee:<br>Deliver me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>171</sup> My lips shall utter praise,<br>When thou hast taught me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>172</sup> My tongue shall speak of thy word:<br>For all thy commandments _are_ righteousness.<br>
<sup>173</sup> Let thine hand help me;<br>For I have chosen thy precepts.<br>
<sup>174</sup> I have longed for thy salvation, O LORD;<br>And thy law _is_ my delight.<br>
<sup>175</sup> Let my soul live, and it shall praise thee;<br>And let thy judgments help me.<br>
<sup>176</sup> I have gone astray like a lost sheep;<br>seek thy servant; For I do not forget thy commandments.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _perfect_, or _sincere_
> - <sup>b</sup> 7. Heb. _judgments of thy righteousness_
> - <sup>c</sup> 18. Heb. _Reveal_
> - <sup>d</sup> 24. Heb. _men of my counsel_
> - <sup>e</sup> 28. Heb. _droppeth_
> - <sup>f</sup> 37. Heb. _make to pass_
> - <sup>g</sup> 45. Heb. _at large_
> - <sup>h</sup> 58. Heb. _face_
> - <sup>i</sup> 61. Or, _companies_
> - <sup>j</sup> 75. Heb. _righteousness_
> - <sup>k</sup> 76. Heb. _to comfort me_
> - <sup>l</sup> 86. Heb. _faithfulness_
> - <sup>m</sup> 90. Heb. _to generation and generation_
> - <sup>n</sup> 90. Heb. _standeth_
> - <sup>o</sup> 98. Heb. _it is ever with me_
> - <sup>p</sup> 103. Heb. _palate_
> - <sup>q</sup> 105. Or, _candle_
> - <sup>r</sup> 112. Heb. _to do_
> - <sup>s</sup> 119. Heb. _causeth to cease_
> - <sup>t</sup> 132. Heb. _according to the custom towards those_, etc.
> - <sup>u</sup> 138. Heb. _righteousness_
> - <sup>v</sup> 138. Heb. _faithfulness_
> - <sup>w</sup> 139. Heb. _cut me off_
> - <sup>x</sup> 140. Heb. _tried_ or _refined_
> - <sup>y</sup> 143. Heb. _found me_
> - <sup>z</sup> 146. Or, _that I may keep_
> - <sup>aa</sup> 156. Or, _many_
> - <sup>ab</sup> 160. Heb. _The beginning of thy word is true_
> - <sup>ac</sup> 165. Heb. _they shall have no stumbling block_

## Chapter 120

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> In my distress<br>I cried unto the LORD, and he heard me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Deliver my soul, O LORD, from lying lips,<br>_And_ from a deceitful tongue.<br>
<sup>3</sup> What shall be given unto thee?<sup>a</sup> or what shall be done<sup>b</sup> unto thee, thou false tongue?<br>
<sup>4</sup> Sharp arrows of the mighty, with coals of juniper.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>5</sup> Woe is me, that I sojourn in Mesech,<br>_That_ I dwell in the tents of Kedar!<br>
<sup>6</sup> My soul hath long dwelt<br>With him that hateth peace.<br>
<sup>7</sup> I _am for_ peace:<sup>d</sup><br>But when I speak, they _are_ for war.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _what shall the deceitful tongue give unto thee?_ or _what shall it profit thee?_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Heb. _added_
> - <sup>c</sup> 4. Or, _It is as the sharp arrows of the mighty man with coals of juniper_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Or, _a man of peace_

## Chapter 121

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> I will lift up mine eyes unto the hills,<br>From whence cometh my help.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>2</sup> My help _cometh_ from the LORD,<br>Which made heaven and earth.<br>
<sup>3</sup> He will not suffer thy foot to be moved:<br>He that keepeth thee will not slumber.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Behold, he that keepeth Israel<br>Shall neither slumber nor sleep.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The LORD _is_ thy keeper:<br>The LORD _is_ thy shade upon thy right hand.<br>
<sup>6</sup> The sun shall not smite thee by day,<br>Nor the moon by night.<br>
<sup>7</sup> The LORD shall preserve thee from all evil:<br>He shall preserve thy soul.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The LORD shall preserve thy going out and thy coming in<br>From this time forth, and even for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _shall I lift up mine eyes to the hills? whence should my help come?_

## Chapter 122

> _A Song of degrees of David._

<sup>1</sup> I was glad when they said unto me,<br>Let us go into the house of the LORD.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Our feet shall stand<br>Within thy gates, O Jerusalem.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Jerusalem is builded<br>As a city that is compact together:<br>
<sup>4</sup> Whither the tribes go up, the tribes of the LORD,<br>Unto the testimony of Israel, to give thanks unto the name of the LORD.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For there are set<sup>a</sup> thrones of judgment,<br>The thrones of the house of David.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Pray for the peace of Jerusalem:<br>They shall prosper that love thee.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Peace be within thy walls,<br>_And_ prosperity within thy palaces.<br>
<sup>8</sup> For my brethren and companions’ sakes,<br>I will now say, Peace _be_ within thee.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Because of the house of the LORD our God<br>I will seek thy good.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Heb. _do set_

## Chapter 123

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> Unto thee lift I up mine eyes,<br>O thou that dwellest in the heavens.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Behold, as the eyes of servants<br>_Look_ unto the hand of their masters, _and_ as the eyes of a maiden unto the hand of her mistress;<br>So our eyes _wait_ upon the LORD our God,<br>Until that he have mercy upon us.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Have mercy upon us, O LORD, have mercy upon us:<br>For we are exceedingly filled with contempt.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Our soul is exceedingly filled with the scorning of those that are at ease,<br>_And_ with the contempt of the proud.<br>

## Chapter 124

> _A Song of degrees of David._

<sup>1</sup> If _it had_ not _been_ the LORD who was on our side, now may Israel say;<br>
<sup>2</sup> If _it had_ not _been_ the LORD who was on our side, when men rose up against us:<br>
<sup>3</sup> Then they had swallowed us up quick,<br>When their wrath was kindled against us:<br>
<sup>4</sup> Then the waters had overwhelmed us,<br>The stream had gone over our soul:<br>
<sup>5</sup> Then the proud waters,<br>Had gone over our soul.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Blessed _be_ the LORD,<br>Who hath not given us _as_ a prey to their teeth.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Our soul is escaped as a bird out of the snare of the fowlers:<br>The snare is broken, and we are escaped.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Our help _is_ in the name of the LORD,<br>Who made heaven and earth.<br>

## Chapter 125

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> They that trust in the LORD<br>_Shall be_ as mount Zion,<br>_Which_ cannot be removed,<br>_But_ abideth for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> _As_ the mountains _are_ round about Jerusalem,<br>So the LORD _is_ round about his people from henceforth even for ever.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For the rod of the wicked<sup>a</sup> shall not rest upon the lot of the righteous;<br>Lest the righteous put forth their hands unto iniquity.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Do good, O LORD,<br>Unto _those that be_ good, and to _them that are_ upright in their hearts.<br>
<sup>5</sup> As for such as turn aside unto their crooked ways, the LORD shall lead them forth with the workers of iniquity:<br>_But_ peace _shall be_ upon Israel.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _wickedness_

## Chapter 126

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> When the LORD turned again the captivity of Zion,<sup>a</sup><br>We were like them that dream.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Then was our mouth filled with laughter,<br>And our tongue with singing:<br>Then said they among the heathen,<br>The LORD hath done great things for them.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>3</sup> The LORD hath done great things for us;<br>_Whereof_ we are glad.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Turn again our captivity, O LORD,<br>As the streams in the south.<br>
<sup>5</sup> They that sow in tears<br>Shall reap in joy.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>6</sup> He that goeth forth and weepeth, bearing precious seed,<sup>d</sup><br>Shall doubtless come again with rejoicing, bringing his sheaves _with him_.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _returned the returning of Zion_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Heb. _hath magnified to do with them_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Or, _singing_
> - <sup>d</sup> 6. Or, _seed basket_

## Chapter 127

> _A Song of degrees for Solomon._

<sup>1</sup> Except the LORD build the house,<br>They labour in vain that build it:<sup>a</sup><br>Except the LORD keep the city,<br>The watchman waketh _but_ in vain.<br>
<sup>2</sup> _It is_ vain for you to rise up early, to sit up late, to eat the bread of sorrows:<br>_For_ so he giveth his beloved sleep.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Lo, children _are_ an heritage of the LORD:<br>_And_ the fruit of the womb _is his_ reward.<br>
<sup>4</sup> As arrows _are_ in the hand of a mighty man;<br>So _are_ children of the youth.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Happy _is_ the man that hath his quiver full of them:<sup>b</sup><br>They shall not be ashamed, but they shall speak<sup>c</sup> with the enemies in the gate.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _are builders of it in it_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _hath filled his quiver with them_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Or, _shall subdue, as Psal. 18 .45._ Or, _destroy_

## Chapter 128

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> Blessed _is_ every one that feareth the LORD;<br>That walketh in his ways.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For thou shalt eat the labour of thine hands:<br>Happy _shalt_ thou _be_, and _it shall be_ well with thee.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thy wife _shall be_ as a fruitful vine by the sides of thine house: Thy children like olive plants round about thy table.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Behold, that thus shall the man be blessed that feareth the LORD.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The LORD shall bless thee out of Zion:<br>And thou shalt see the good of Jerusalem all the days of thy life.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Yea, thou shalt see thy children’s children,<br>_And_ peace upon Israel.<br>

## Chapter 129

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> Many<sup>a</sup> a time have they afflicted me from my youth,<br>May Israel now say:<br>
<sup>2</sup> Many a time have they afflicted me from my youth:<br>Yet they have not prevailed against me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The plowers plowed upon my back:<br>They made long their furrows.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The LORD _is_ righteous:<br>He hath cut asunder the cords of the wicked.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let them all be confounded and turned back<br>That hate Zion.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Let them be as the grass _upon_ the housetops,<br>Which withereth afore it groweth up:<br>
<sup>7</sup> Wherewith the mower filleth not his hand;<br>Nor he that bindeth sheaves his bosom.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Neither do they which go by say, the blessing of the LORD _be_ upon you:<br>We bless you in the name of the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _much_

## Chapter 130

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> Out of the depths have I cried unto thee, O LORD.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Lord, hear my voice:<br>Let thine ears be attentive<br>To the voice of my supplications.<br>
<sup>3</sup> If thou, LORD, shouldest mark iniquities,<br>O Lord, who shall stand?<br>
<sup>4</sup> But _there is_ forgiveness with thee,<br>That thou mayest be feared.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I wait for the LORD, my soul doth wait,<br>And in his word do I hope.<br>
<sup>6</sup> My soul _waiteth_ for the Lord<br>More than they that watch for the morning: _I say, more than_ they that watch for the morning.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Let Israel hope in the LORD: For with the LORD _there is_ mercy,<br>And with him _is_ plenteous redemption.<br>
<sup>8</sup> And he shall redeem Israel from all his iniquities.<br>

## Chapter 131

> _A Song of degrees of David._

<sup>1</sup> LORD, my heart is not haughty,<br>Nor mine eyes lofty:<br>Neither do I exercise<sup>a</sup> myself in<br>Great matters, or in things too high<sup>b</sup> for me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Surely I have behaved and quieted myself,<sup>c</sup><br>As a child that is weaned of his mother: My soul _is_ even as a weaned child.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Let Israel hope in the LORD<br>From henceforth<sup>d</sup> and for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _walk_
> - <sup>b</sup> 1. Heb. _wonderful_
> - <sup>c</sup> 2. Heb. _my soul_
> - <sup>d</sup> 3. Heb. _from now_

## Chapter 132

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> LORD, remember David,<br>_And_ all his afflictions:<br>
<sup>2</sup> How he sware unto the LORD,<br>_And_ vowed unto the mighty _God_ of Jacob;<br>
<sup>3</sup> Surely I will not come into the tabernacle of my house,<br>Nor go up into my bed;<br>
<sup>4</sup> I will not give sleep to mine eyes, _or_ slumber to mine eyelids,<br>
<sup>5</sup> Until I find out a place for the LORD,<br>An habitation<sup>a</sup> for the mighty _God_ of Jacob.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Lo, we heard of it at Ephratah:<br>We found it in the fields of the wood.<br>
<sup>7</sup> We will go into his tabernacles:<br>We will worship at his footstool.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Arise, O LORD, into thy rest;<br>Thou, and the ark of thy strength.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Let thy priests be clothed with righteousness;<br>And let thy saints shout for joy.<br>
<sup>10</sup> For thy servant David’s sake<br>Turn not away the face of thine anointed.<br>
<sup>11</sup> The LORD hath sworn _in_ truth unto David;<br>He will not turn from it;<br>Of the fruit of thy body<sup>b</sup><br>Will I set upon thy throne.<br>
<sup>12</sup> If thy children will keep my covenant and my testimony that I shall teach them,<br>Their children shall also sit upon thy throne for evermore.<br>
<sup>13</sup> For the LORD hath chosen Zion;<br>He hath desired _it_ for his habitation.<br>
<sup>14</sup> This _is_ my rest for ever:<br>Here will I dwell; for I have desired it.<br>
<sup>15</sup> I will abundantly<sup>c</sup> bless her provision:<br>I will satisfy her poor with bread.<br>
<sup>16</sup> I will also clothe her priests with salvation:<br>And her saints shall shout aloud for joy.<br>
<sup>17</sup> There will I make the horn of David to bud:<br>I have ordained a lamp<sup>d</sup> for mine anointed.<br>
<sup>18</sup> His enemies will I clothe with shame:<br>But upon himself shall his crown flourish.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Heb. _habitations_
> - <sup>b</sup> 11. Heb. _thy belly_
> - <sup>c</sup> 15. Or, _surely_
> - <sup>d</sup> 17. Or, _candle_

## Chapter 133

> _A Song of degrees of David._

<sup>1</sup> Behold, how good and how pleasant _it is_<br>For brethren to dwell together<sup>a</sup> in unity!<br>
<sup>2</sup> _It is_ like the precious ointment upon the head, that ran down upon the beard,<br>_Even_ Aaron’s beard: That went down to the skirts of his garments;<br>
<sup>3</sup> As the dew of Hermon,<br>_And as the dew_ that descended upon the mountains of Zion:<br>For there the LORD commanded the blessing,<br>_Even_ life for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _even together_

## Chapter 134

> _A Song of degrees._

<sup>1</sup> Behold, bless ye the LORD,<br>All _ye_ servants of the LORD,<br>Which by night stand in the house of the LORD.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Lift up your hands _in_ the sanctuary,<sup>a</sup><br>And bless the LORD.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The LORD that made heaven and earth<br>Bless thee out of Zion.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _in holiness_

## Chapter 135

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye the LORD. Praise ye the name of the LORD;<br>Praise _him_, O ye servants of the LORD.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Ye that stand in the house of the LORD,<br>In the courts of the house of our God,<br>
<sup>3</sup> Praise the LORD; for the LORD _is_ good:<br>Sing praises unto his name; for _it is_ pleasant.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For the LORD hath chosen Jacob unto himself,<br>_And_ Israel for his peculiar treasure.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For I know that the LORD _is_ great,<br>And _that_ our Lord _is_ above all gods.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Whatsoever the LORD pleased, _that_ did he in heaven, and in earth,<br>In the seas, and all deep places.<br>
<sup>7</sup> He causeth the vapours to ascend from the ends of the earth;<br>He maketh lightnings for the rain; He bringeth the wind out of his treasuries.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Who smote the firstborn of Egypt,<br>Both of man and beast.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>9</sup> _Who_ sent tokens and wonders into the midst of thee, O Egypt,<br>Upon Pharaoh, and upon all his servants.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Who smote great nations,<br>And slew mighty kings;<br>
<sup>11</sup> Sihon king of the Amorites, and Og king of Bashan,<br>And all the kingdoms of Canaan:<br>
<sup>12</sup> And gave their land _for_ an heritage,<br>An heritage unto Israel his people.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Thy name, O LORD, _endureth_ for ever;<br>_And_ thy memorial, O LORD, throughout all generations.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>14</sup> For the LORD will judge his people,<br>And he will repent himself concerning his servants.<br>
<sup>15</sup> The idols of the heathen<br>_are_ silver and gold, the work of men’s hands.<br>
<sup>16</sup> They have mouths, but they speak not;<br>Eyes have they, but they see not;<br>
<sup>17</sup> They have ears, but they hear not;<br>Neither is there _any_ breath in their mouths.<br>
<sup>18</sup> They that make them are like unto them:<br>_So is_ every one that trusteth in them.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Bless the LORD, O house of Israel:<br>Bless the LORD, O house of Aaron:<br>
<sup>20</sup> Bless the LORD, O house of Levi:<br>Ye that fear the LORD, bless the LORD.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Blessed be the LORD out of Zion,<br>Which dwelleth at Jerusalem.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 8. Heb. _from man unto beast_
> - <sup>b</sup> 13. Heb. _to generation and generation_

## Chapter 136

<sup>1</sup> O give thanks unto the LORD; for _he is_ good:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> O give thanks unto the God of gods:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>3</sup> O give thanks to the Lord of lords:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>4</sup> To him who alone doeth great wonders:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>5</sup> To him that by wisdom made the heavens:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>6</sup> To him that stretched out the earth above the waters:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>7</sup> To him that made great lights:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>8</sup> The sun to rule by day:<sup>a</sup><br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>9</sup> The moon and stars to rule by night:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>10</sup> To him that smote Egypt in their firstborn:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>11</sup> And brought out Israel from among them:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>12</sup> With a strong hand, and with a stretched out arm:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>13</sup> To him which divided the Red sea into parts:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>14</sup> And made Israel to pass through the midst of it:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>15</sup> But overthrew<sup>b</sup> Pharaoh and his host in the Red sea:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>16</sup> To him which led his people through the wilderness:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>17</sup> To him which smote great kings:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>18</sup> And slew famous kings:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>19</sup> Sihon king of the Amorites:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>20</sup> And Og the king of Bashan:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>21</sup> And gave their land for an heritage:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>22</sup> _Even_ an heritage unto Israel his servant:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Who remembered us in our low estate:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>24</sup> And hath redeemed us from our enemies:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>25</sup> Who giveth food to all flesh:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>26</sup> O give thanks unto the God of heaven:<br>For his mercy _endureth_ for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 8. Heb. _for the rulings by day_
> - <sup>b</sup> 15. Heb. _shaked off_

## Chapter 137

<sup>1</sup> By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat down, yea, we wept,<br>When we remembered Zion.<br>
<sup>2</sup> We hanged our harps<br>Upon the willows in the midst thereof.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For there they that carried us away captive required of us a song;<sup>a</sup> And they that wasted us<sup>b</sup> _required of us_ mirth, _saying_,<br>Sing us _one_ of the songs of Zion.<br>
<sup>4</sup> How shall we sing<br>The LORD’s song in a strange land?<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>5</sup> If I forget thee, O Jerusalem,<br>Let my right hand forget _her cunning_.<br>
<sup>6</sup> If I do not remember thee, let my tongue cleave to the roof of my mouth;<br>If I prefer not Jerusalem above my chief joy.<sup>d</sup><br>
<sup>7</sup> Remember, O LORD, the children of Edom in the day of Jerusalem;<br>Who said, Rase _it_, rase _it_, _even_ to the foundation thereof.<br>
<sup>8</sup> O daughter of Babylon, who art to be destroyed;<sup>e</sup><br>Happy _shall he be_, that rewardeth thee as thou hast served us.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Happy _shall he be_, that taketh and dasheth thy little ones<br>Against the stones.<sup>f</sup><br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _the words of a song_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Heb. _laid us on heaps_
> - <sup>c</sup> 4. Heb. _land of a stranger_
> - <sup>d</sup> 6. Heb. _the head of my joy_
> - <sup>e</sup> 8. Heb. _wasted_
> - <sup>f</sup> 9. Heb. _the rock_

## Chapter 138

> __A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> I will praise thee with my whole heart:<br>Before the gods will I sing praise unto thee.<br>
<sup>2</sup> I will worship toward thy holy temple, and praise thy name for thy lovingkindness and for thy truth:<br>For thou hast magnified thy word above all thy name.<br>
<sup>3</sup> In the day when I cried thou answeredst me,<br>_And_ strengthenedst me _with_ strength in my soul.<br>
<sup>4</sup> All the kings of the earth shall praise thee, O LORD,<br>When they hear the words of thy mouth.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Yea, they shall sing in the ways of the LORD:<br>For great _is_ the glory of the LORD.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Though the LORD _be_ high, yet hath he respect unto the lowly:<br>But the proud he knoweth afar off.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Though I walk in the midst of trouble, thou wilt revive me:<br>Thou shalt stretch forth thine hand against the wrath of mine enemies, and thy right hand shall save me.<br>
<sup>8</sup> The LORD will perfect _that which_ concerneth me:<br>Thy mercy, O LORD, _endureth_ for ever: Forsake not the works of thine own hands.<br>

## Chapter 139

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> O LORD, thou hast searched me, and known _me_.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Thou knowest my downsitting and mine uprising, thou understandest my thought afar off.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thou compassest<sup>a</sup> my path and my lying down,<br>And art acquainted _with_ all my ways.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For _there is_ not a word in my tongue,<br>_But_, lo, O LORD, thou knowest it altogether.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Thou hast beset me behind and before,<br>And laid thine hand upon me.<br>
<sup>6</sup> _Such_ knowledge _is_ too wonderful for me;<br>It is high, I cannot _attain_ unto it.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Whither shall I go from thy spirit?<br>Or whither shall I flee from thy presence?<br>
<sup>8</sup> If I ascend up into heaven, thou _art_ there:<br>If I make my bed in hell, behold, thou _art there_.<br>
<sup>9</sup> _If_ I take the wings of the morning,<br>_And_ dwell in the uttermost parts of the sea;<br>
<sup>10</sup> Even there shall thy hand lead me,<br>And thy right hand shall hold me.<br>
<sup>11</sup> If I say, Surely the darkness shall cover me;<br>Even the night shall be light about me.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Yea, the darkness hideth not<sup>b</sup> from thee; But the night shineth as the day:<br>The darkness and the light<sup>c</sup> _are_ both alike _to thee_.<br>
<sup>13</sup> For thou hast possessed my reins:<br>Thou hast covered me in my mother’s womb.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I will praise thee; for I am fearfully _and_ wonderfully made:<br>Marvellous _are_ thy works; And _that_ my soul knoweth right<sup>d</sup> well.<br>
<sup>15</sup> My substance<sup>e</sup> was not hid from thee,<br>When I was made in secret, _and_ curiously wrought in the lowest parts of the earth.<br>
<sup>16</sup> Thine eyes did see my substance, yet being unperfect;<br>And in thy book all _my members_<sup>f</sup> were written,<br>_Which_ in continuance were fashioned,<sup>g</sup><br>When _as yet there was_ none of them.<br>
<sup>17</sup> How precious also are thy thoughts unto me, O God!<br>How great is the sum of them!<br>
<sup>18</sup> _If_ I should count them, they are more in number than the sand:<br>When I awake, I am still with thee.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Surely thou wilt slay the wicked, O God:<br>Depart from me therefore, ye bloody men.<br>
<sup>20</sup> For they speak against thee wickedly,<br>_And_ thine enemies take _thy name_ in vain.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Do not I hate them, O LORD, that hate thee?<br>And am not I grieved with those that rise up against thee?<br>
<sup>22</sup> I hate them with perfect hatred:<br>I count them mine enemies.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Search me, O God, and know my heart:<br>Try me, and know my thoughts:<br>
<sup>24</sup> And see if _there be any_ wicked way<sup>h</sup> in me,<br>And lead me in the way everlasting.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _winnowest_
> - <sup>b</sup> 12. Heb. _darkeneth not_
> - <sup>c</sup> 12. Heb. _as is the darkness so is the light_
> - <sup>d</sup> 14. Heb. _greatly_
> - <sup>e</sup> 15. Or, _strength_ or _body_
> - <sup>f</sup> 16. Heb. _all of them_
> - <sup>g</sup> 16. Or, _what days they should be fashioned_
> - <sup>h</sup> 24. Heb. _way of pain_, or _grief_

## Chapter 140

> _To the chief Musician, A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Deliver me, O LORD, from the evil man:<br>Preserve me from the violent man;<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>2</sup> Which imagine mischiefs in _their_ heart;<br>Continually are they gathered together _for_ war.<br>
<sup>3</sup> They have sharpened their tongues like a serpent;<br>Adders’ poison _is_ under their lips. _Selah._<br>
<sup>4</sup> Keep me, O LORD, from the hands of the wicked;<br>Preserve me from the violent man; Who have purposed to overthrow my goings.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The proud have hid a snare for me, and cords; They have spread a net by the wayside;<br>They have set gins for me. _Selah._<br>
<sup>6</sup> I said unto the LORD, Thou _art_ my God:<br>Hear the voice of my supplications, O LORD.<br>
<sup>7</sup> O GOD the Lord, the strength of my salvation,<br>Thou hast covered my head in the day of battle.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Grant not, O LORD, the desires of the wicked:<br>Further not his wicked device; _lest_ they exalt themselves.<sup>b</sup> _Selah._<br>
<sup>9</sup> _As for_ the head of those that compass me about,<br>Let the mischief of their own lips cover them.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let burning coals fall upon them:<br>Let them be cast into the fire; Into deep pits, that they rise not up again.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Let not an evil speaker be established in the earth:<br>Evil shall hunt the violent man to overthrow _him_.<br>
<sup>12</sup> I know that the LORD will maintain the cause of the afflicted,<br>_And_ the right of the poor.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Surely the righteous shall give thanks unto thy name:<br>The upright shall dwell in thy presence.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _man of violences_
> - <sup>b</sup> 8. Or, _let them not be exalted_

## Chapter 141

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> LORD, I cry unto thee: make haste unto me;<br>Give ear unto my voice, when I cry unto thee.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let my prayer be set forth before thee _as_ incense;<br>_And_ the lifting up of my hands _as_ the evening sacrifice.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Set a watch, O LORD, before my mouth;<br>Keep the door of my lips.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Incline not my heart to _any_ evil thing,<br>To practise wicked works with men that work iniquity: And let me not eat of their dainties.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let the righteous smite me;<br>_It shall be_ a kindness: And let him reprove me;<br>_It shall be_ an excellent oil, _which_ shall not break my head:<br>For yet my prayer also _shall be_ in their calamities.<br>
<sup>6</sup> When their judges are overthrown in stony places,<br>They shall hear my words; for they are sweet.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Our bones are scattered at the grave’s mouth,<br>As when one cutteth and cleaveth _wood_ upon the earth.<br>
<sup>8</sup> But mine eyes _are_ unto thee, O GOD the Lord:<br>In thee is my trust; leave not my soul destitute.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>9</sup> Keep me from the snares _which_ they have laid for me, and the gins of the workers of iniquity.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let the wicked fall into their own nets, whilst that I withal escape.<sup>b</sup><br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 8. Heb. _make not my soul bare_
> - <sup>b</sup> 10. Heb. _pass over_

## Chapter 142

> _Maschil of David; A Prayer when he was in the cave._

<sup>1</sup> I cried unto the LORD with my voice;<br>With my voice unto the LORD did I make my supplication.<br>
<sup>2</sup> I poured out my complaint before him;<br>I shewed before him my trouble.<br>
<sup>3</sup> When my spirit was overwhelmed within me, then thou knewest my path.<br>In the way wherein I walked have they privily laid a snare for me.<br>
<sup>4</sup> I looked on _my_ right hand,<br>And beheld,<sup>a</sup> but _there was_ no man that would know me:<br>Refuge failed me;<sup>b</sup><br>No man cared for my soul.<sup>c</sup><br>
<sup>5</sup> I cried unto thee, O LORD:<br>I said, Thou _art_ my refuge _and_ my portion in the land of the living.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Attend unto my cry;<br>For I am brought very low:<br>Deliver me from my persecutors;<br>For they are stronger than I.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Bring my soul out of prison,<br>That I may praise thy name:<br>The righteous shall compass me about;<br>For thou shalt deal bountifully with me.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Or, _Look on the right hand, and see_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Heb. _perished from me_
> - <sup>c</sup> 4. Heb. _no man sought after my soul_

## Chapter 143

> _A Psalm of David._

<sup>1</sup> Hear my prayer, O LORD, give ear to my supplications:<br>In thy faithfulness answer me, _and_ in thy righteousness.<br>
<sup>2</sup> And enter not into judgment with thy servant:<br>For in thy sight shall no man living be justified.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For the enemy hath persecuted my soul; He hath smitten my life down to the ground;<br>He hath made me to dwell in darkness, as those that have been long dead.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Therefore is my spirit overwhelmed within me;<br>My heart within me is desolate.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I remember the days of old; I meditate on all thy works;<br>I muse on the work of thy hands.<br>
<sup>6</sup> I stretch forth my hands unto thee:<br>My soul _thirsteth_ after thee, as a thirsty land. _Selah._<br>
<sup>7</sup> Hear me speedily, O LORD: my spirit faileth:<br>Hide not thy face from me, lest I be like<sup>a</sup> unto them that go down into the pit.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Cause me to hear thy lovingkindness in the morning; For in thee do I trust:<br>Cause me to know the way wherein I should walk; For I lift up my soul unto thee.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Deliver me, O LORD, from mine enemies:<br>I flee unto thee to hide me.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Teach me to do thy will; for thou _art_ my God:<br>Thy spirit _is_ good; lead me into the land of uprightness.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Quicken me, O LORD, for thy name’s sake:<br>For thy righteousness’ sake bring my soul out of trouble.<br>
<sup>12</sup> And of thy mercy cut off mine enemies,<br>And destroy all them that afflict my soul: For I _am_ thy servant.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 7. Or, _for I am become like._ etc.

## Chapter 144

> __A Psalm_ of David._

<sup>1</sup> Blessed _be_ the LORD my strength,<sup>a</sup><br>Which teacheth my hands to war,<sup>b</sup> _and_ my fingers to fight:<br>
<sup>2</sup> My goodness,<sup>c</sup> and my fortress; My high tower, and my deliverer; My shield, and _he_ in whom I trust;<br>Who subdueth my people under me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> LORD, what _is_ man, that thou takest knowledge of him!<br>_Or_ the son of man, that thou makest account of him!<br>
<sup>4</sup> Man is like to vanity:<br>His days _are_ as a shadow that passeth away.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Bow thy heavens, O LORD, and come down:<br>Touch the mountains, and they shall smoke.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Cast forth lightning, and scatter them:<br>Shoot out thine arrows, and destroy them.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Send thine hand<sup>d</sup> from above;<br>Rid me, and deliver me out of great waters, from the hand of strange children;<br>
<sup>8</sup> Whose mouth speaketh vanity,<br>And their right hand _is_ a right hand of falsehood.<br>
<sup>9</sup> I will sing a new song unto thee, O God:<br>Upon a psaltery _and_ an instrument of ten strings will I sing praises unto thee.<br>
<sup>10</sup> _It is he_ that giveth salvation<sup>e</sup> unto kings:<br>Who delivereth David his servant from the hurtful sword.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Rid me, and deliver me from the hand of strange children,<br>Whose mouth speaketh vanity, and their right hand _is_ a right hand of falsehood:<br>
<sup>12</sup> That our sons _may be_ as plants grown up in their youth;<br>_That_ our daughters _may be_ as corner stones, polished<sup>f</sup> _after_ the similitude of a palace:<br>
<sup>13</sup> _That_ our garners _may be_ full, affording all manner of store:<sup>g</sup><br>_That_ our sheep may bring forth thousands and ten thousands in our streets:<br>
<sup>14</sup> _That_ our oxen _may be_ strong to labour;<sup>h</sup><br>_That there be_ no breaking in, nor going out; That _there be_ no complaining in our streets.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Happy _is that_ people, that is in such a case:<br>_Yea_, happy _is that_ people, whose God _is_ the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _my rock_
> - <sup>b</sup> 1. Heb. _to the war_, etc.
> - <sup>c</sup> 2. Or, _my mercy_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Heb. _hands_
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Or, _victory_
> - <sup>f</sup> 12. Heb. _cut_
> - <sup>g</sup> 13. Heb. _from kind to kind_
> - <sup>h</sup> 14. Heb. _able to bear burdens_, or _loaden with flesh_

## Chapter 145

> _David’s _Psalm_ of praise._

#### א Aleph

<sup>1</sup> I will extol thee, my God, O King;<br>And I will bless thy name for ever and ever.<br>

#### ב Bet
<sup>2</sup> Every day will I bless thee;<br>And I will praise thy name for ever and ever.<br>

#### ג Gimel
<sup>3</sup> Great _is_ the LORD, and greatly to be praised;<br>And his greatness _is_ unsearchable.<sup>a</sup><br>

#### ד Dalet
<sup>4</sup> One generation shall praise thy works to another,<br>And shall declare thy mighty acts.<br>

#### ה He
<sup>5</sup> I will speak of the glorious honour of thy majesty,<br>And of thy wondrous works.<sup>b</sup><br>

#### ו Waw
<sup>6</sup> And _men_ shall speak of the might of thy terrible acts:<br>And I will declare<sup>c</sup> thy greatness.<br>

#### ז Zain
<sup>7</sup> They shall abundantly utter the memory of thy great goodness,<br>And shall sing of thy righteousness.<br>

#### ח Het
<sup>8</sup> The LORD _is_ gracious, and full of compassion;<br>Slow to anger, and of great mercy.<sup>d</sup><br>

#### ט Tet
<sup>9</sup> The LORD _is_ good to all:<br>And his tender mercies _are_ over all his works.<br>

#### י Yod
<sup>10</sup> All thy works shall praise thee, O LORD;<br>And thy saints shall bless thee.<br>

#### כ Kaph
<sup>11</sup> They shall speak of the glory of thy kingdom,<br>And talk of thy power;<br>

#### ל Lamed
<sup>12</sup> To make known to the sons of men his mighty acts,<br>And the glorious majesty of his kingdom.<br>

#### מ Mem
<sup>13</sup> Thy kingdom _is_ an everlasting kingdom,<sup>e</sup><br>And thy dominion _endureth_ throughout all generations.<br>

#### ס Samek
<sup>14</sup> The LORD upholdeth all that fall,<br>And raiseth up all _those that be_ bowed down.<br>

#### ע Ain
<sup>15</sup> The eyes of all wait upon thee;<sup>f</sup><br>And thou givest them their meat in due season.<br>

#### פ Pe
<sup>16</sup> Thou openest thine hand,<br>And satisfiest the desire of every living thing.<br>

#### צ Zade
<sup>17</sup> The LORD _is_ righteous in all his ways,<br>And holy<sup>g</sup> in all his works.<br>

#### ק Qoph
<sup>18</sup> The LORD _is_ nigh unto all them that call upon him,<br>To all that call upon him in truth.<br>

#### ר Resh
<sup>19</sup> He will fulfil the desire of them that fear him:<br>He also will hear their cry, and will save them.<br>

#### ש Shin
<sup>20</sup> The LORD preserveth all them that love him:<br>But all the wicked will he destroy.<br>

#### ת Taw
<sup>21</sup> My mouth shall speak the praise of the LORD:<br>And let all flesh bless his holy name for ever and ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _and of his greatness there is no search_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _things_ or _words_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Heb. _declare it_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Heb. _great in mercy_
> - <sup>e</sup> 13. Heb. _a kingdom of all ages_
> - <sup>f</sup> 15. Or, _look unto thee_
> - <sup>g</sup> 17. Or, _merciful_, or _bountiful_

## Chapter 146

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD. Praise the LORD, O my soul.<br>
<sup>2</sup> While I live will I praise the LORD: I will sing praises unto my God while I have any being.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Put not your trust in princes, _nor_ in the son of man,<br>In whom _there is_ no help.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>4</sup> His breath goeth forth, he returneth to his earth;<br>In that very day his thoughts perish.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Happy _is he_ that _hath_ the God of Jacob for his help,<br>Whose hope _is_ in the LORD his God:<br>
<sup>6</sup> Which made heaven, and earth, the sea, and all that therein _is:_<br>Which keepeth truth for ever:<br>
<sup>7</sup> Which executeth judgment for the oppressed:<br>Which giveth food to the hungry.<br>The LORD looseth the prisoners:<br>
<sup>8</sup> The LORD openeth _the eyes of_ the blind:<br>The LORD raiseth them that are bowed down:<br>The LORD loveth the righteous:<br>
