<sup>9</sup> The LORD preserveth the strangers; He relieveth the fatherless and widow:<br>But the way of the wicked he turneth upside down.<br>
<sup>10</sup> The LORD shall reign for ever, _even_ thy God, O Zion,<br>Unto all generations.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Or, _salvation_

## Chapter 147

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye the LORD: For _it is_ good to sing praises unto our God;<br>For _it is_ pleasant; _and_ praise is comely.<br>
<sup>2</sup> The LORD doth build up Jerusalem:<br>He gathereth together the outcasts of Israel.<br>
<sup>3</sup> He healeth the broken in heart,<br>And bindeth up their wounds.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>4</sup> He telleth the number of the stars;<br>He calleth them all by _their_ names.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Great _is_ our Lord, and of great power:<br>His understanding _is_ infinite.<sup>b</sup><br>
<sup>6</sup> The LORD lifteth up the meek:<br>He casteth the wicked down to the ground.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Sing unto the LORD with thanksgiving;<br>Sing praise upon the harp unto our God:<br>
<sup>8</sup> Who covereth the heaven with clouds, who prepareth rain for the earth,<br>Who maketh grass to grow upon the mountains.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He giveth to the beast his food,<br>_And_ to the young ravens which cry.<br>
<sup>10</sup> He delighteth not in the strength of the horse:<br>He taketh not pleasure in the legs of a man.<br>
<sup>11</sup> The LORD taketh pleasure in them that fear him,<br>In those that hope in his mercy.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Praise the LORD, O Jerusalem;<br>Praise thy God, O Zion.<br>
<sup>13</sup> For he hath strengthened the bars of thy gates;<br>He hath blessed thy children within thee.<br>
<sup>14</sup> He maketh peace _in_ thy borders,<sup>c</sup><br>_And_ filleth thee with the finest of the wheat.<br>
<sup>15</sup> He sendeth forth his commandment _upon_ earth:<br>His word runneth very swiftly.<br>
<sup>16</sup> He giveth snow like wool:<br>He scattereth the hoarfrost like ashes.<br>
<sup>17</sup> He casteth forth his ice like morsels:<br>Who can stand before his cold?<br>
<sup>18</sup> He sendeth out his word, and melteth them:<br>He causeth his wind to blow, _and_ the waters flow.<br>
<sup>19</sup> He sheweth his word<sup>d</sup> unto Jacob,<br>His statutes and his judgments unto Israel.<br>
<sup>20</sup> He hath not dealt so with any nation:<br>And _as for his_ judgments, they have not known them.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Heb. _griefs_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Heb. _of his understanding there is no number_
> - <sup>c</sup> 14. Heb. _who maketh thy border peace_
> - <sup>d</sup> 19. Heb. _his words_

## Chapter 148

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD.<br>Praise ye the LORD from the heavens:<br>Praise him in the heights.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Praise ye him, all his angels:<br>Praise ye him, all his hosts.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Praise ye him, sun and moon:<br>Praise him, all ye stars of light.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Praise him, ye heavens of heavens,<br>And ye waters that _be_ above the heavens.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let them praise the name of the LORD:<br>For he commanded, and they were created.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He hath also stablished them for ever and ever:<br>He hath made a decree which shall not pass.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Praise the LORD from the earth,<br>Ye dragons, and all deeps:<br>
<sup>8</sup> Fire, and hail; snow, and vapours;<br>Stormy wind fulfilling his word:<br>
<sup>9</sup> Mountains, and all hills;<br>Fruitful trees, and all cedars:<br>
<sup>10</sup> Beasts, and all cattle;<br>Creeping things, and flying fowl:<br>
<sup>11</sup> Kings of the earth, and all people;<br>Princes, and all judges of the earth:<br>
<sup>12</sup> Both young men, and maidens;<br>Old men, and children:<br>
<sup>13</sup> Let them praise the name of the LORD:<br>For his name alone is excellent;<sup>b</sup><br>His glory _is_ above the earth and heaven.<br>
<sup>14</sup> He also exalteth the horn of his people,<br>The praise of all his saints;<br>_Even_ of the children of Israel, a people near unto him.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 13. Heb. _exalted_

## Chapter 149

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD. Sing unto the LORD a new song,<br>_And_ his praise in the congregation of saints.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let Israel rejoice in him that made him:<br>Let the children of Zion be joyful in their King.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Let them praise his name in the dance:<sup>b</sup><br>Let them sing praises unto him with the timbrel and harp.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For the LORD taketh pleasure in his people:<br>He will beautify the meek with salvation.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let the saints be joyful in glory:<br>Let them sing aloud upon their beds.<br>
<sup>6</sup> _Let_ the high _praises_ of God _be_ in their mouth,<sup>c</sup><br>And a twoedged sword in their hand;<br>
<sup>7</sup> To execute vengeance upon the heathen,<br>_And_ punishments upon the people;<br>
<sup>8</sup> To bind their kings with chains,<br>And their nobles with fetters of iron;<br>
<sup>9</sup> To execute upon them the judgment written:<br>This honour have all his saints.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Or, _with the pipe_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Heb. _in their throat_

## Chapter 150

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye<sup>a</sup> the LORD. Praise God in his sanctuary:<br>Praise him in the firmament of his power.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Praise him for his mighty acts:<br>Praise him according to his excellent greatness.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Praise him with the sound of the trumpet:<sup>b</sup><br>Praise him with the psaltery and harp.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Praise him with the timbrel and dance:<sup>c</sup><br>Praise him with stringed instruments and organs.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Praise him upon the loud cymbals:<br>Praise him upon the high sounding cymbals.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Let every thing that hath breath<br>Praise the LORD.<br>Praise ye the LORD.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Heb. _Hallelujah_
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Or, _Cornet_
> - <sup>c</sup> 4. Or, _Pipe_
